```cpp
// A patch site in the stock age3y.exe: its address and the bytes expected there.
#pragma once

#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>

namespace uhc {

struct Site {
  const char* name;
  std::uint32_t va;
  const std::uint8_t* bytes;
  std::size_t size;
};

}  // namespace uhc
```

## Patching

### src/patch.h

```cpp
// In-process patching of the game image. Every write is preceded by a check that the bytes at
// each site are the stock bytes (src/generated/sites.h), so a wrong build is never patched.
#pragma once

#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <initializer_list>

#include "site.h"

namespace uhc {

// True if live memory at the site equals its expected stock bytes.
bool Matches(const Site& site);

// Checks every site; logs and returns false on the first mismatch.
bool VerifyAll(const char* feature, std::initializer_list<const Site*> sites);

bool WriteBytes(std::uint32_t va, const void* data, std::size_t size);
bool WriteDword(std::uint32_t va, std::uint32_t value);
// Writes `jmp rel32` to target at va and NOP-fills the rest of `length` (>= 5) bytes.
bool WriteJmp(std::uint32_t va, const void* target, std::size_t length);
bool WriteJmp(std::uint32_t va, std::uint32_t target, std::size_t length);
// Writes `call rel32` to target at va (5 bytes), e.g. to redirect an existing call.
bool WriteCall(std::uint32_t va, const void* target);

}  // namespace uhc
```

### src/patch.cpp

```cpp
#include "patch.h"

#include <windows.h>

#include <cstring>

#include "log.h"

namespace uhc {
namespace {

void* Ptr(std::uint32_t va) { return reinterpret_cast<void*>(static_cast<std::uintptr_t>(va)); }

}  // namespace

bool Matches(const Site& site) { return std::memcmp(Ptr(site.va), site.bytes, site.size) == 0; }

bool VerifyAll(const char* feature, std::initializer_list<const Site*> sites) {
  for (const Site* site : sites) {
    if (!Matches(*site)) {
      Log(L"%hs: site %hs at 0x%08X does not hold the stock bytes; feature not applied", feature,
          site->name, site->va);
      return false;
    }
  }
  return true;
}

bool WriteBytes(std::uint32_t va, const void* data, std::size_t size) {
  DWORD old = 0;
  if (!VirtualProtect(Ptr(va), size, PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE, &old)) {
    Log(L"VirtualProtect failed at 0x%08X (error %lu)", va, GetLastError());
    return false;
  }
  std::memcpy(Ptr(va), data, size);
  VirtualProtect(Ptr(va), size, old, &old);
  FlushInstructionCache(GetCurrentProcess(), Ptr(va), size);
  return true;
}

bool WriteDword(std::uint32_t va, std::uint32_t value) { return WriteBytes(va, &value, sizeof(value)); }

bool WriteJmp(std::uint32_t va, std::uint32_t target, std::size_t length) {
  if (length < 5 || length > 64) {
    return false;
  }
  std::uint8_t code[64];
  std::memset(code, 0x90, length);
  code[0] = 0xE9;
  const std::uint32_t rel = target - (va + 5);
  std::memcpy(code + 1, &rel, sizeof(rel));
  return WriteBytes(va, code, length);
}

bool WriteJmp(std::uint32_t va, const void* target, std::size_t length) {
  return WriteJmp(va, static_cast<std::uint32_t>(reinterpret_cast<std::uintptr_t>(target)), length);
}

bool WriteCall(std::uint32_t va, const void* target) {
  std::uint8_t code[5];
  code[0] = 0xE8;
  const std::uint32_t rel = static_cast<std::uint32_t>(reinterpret_cast<std::uintptr_t>(target)) - (va + 5);
  std::memcpy(code + 1, &rel, sizeof(rel));
  return WriteBytes(va, code, sizeof(code));
}

}  // namespace uhc
```

## Log

### src/log.h

```cpp
// Append-only log at UHC.log next to the game executable.
#pragma once

namespace uhc {

void Log(const wchar_t* format, ...);

}  // namespace uhc
```

### src/log.cpp

```cpp
#include "log.h"

#include <windows.h>

#include <cstdarg>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cwchar>

#include "hostfiles.h"

namespace uhc {

void Log(const wchar_t* format, ...) {
  wchar_t path[MAX_PATH];
  const DWORD len = GetModuleFileNameW(nullptr, path, MAX_PATH);
  if (len == 0 || len == MAX_PATH) {
    return;
  }
  wchar_t* slash = std::wcsrchr(path, L'\\');
  if (slash == nullptr) {
    return;
  }
  wcscpy_s(slash + 1, MAX_PATH - (slash + 1 - path), kLogFile);

  FILE* file = nullptr;
  if (_wfopen_s(&file, path, L"a, ccs=UTF-8") != 0 || file == nullptr) {
    return;
  }
  va_list args;
  va_start(args, format);
  std::vfwprintf(file, format, args);
  va_end(args);
  std::fputwc(L'\n', file);
  std::fclose(file);
}

}  // namespace uhc

// Called once per hook by TRACE_ONCE (hooks/common.inc), so a test run shows which patched
// code paths the game actually executed.
extern "C" void __stdcall UhcTraceHook(const char* hookName) {
  uhc::Log(L"hook reached: %hs", hookName);
}
```

### src/hooks/common.inc

```asm
; Shared macros for the hook files.

UhcTraceHook PROTO STDCALL hookName:DWORD

; Logs "hook reached: <text>" to UHC.log the first time execution passes here. Preserves every
; register and the flags (some hooks branch on the flags they are entered with), and leaves
; ESP as it found it, so it can sit before any stack-relative access.
TRACE_ONCE MACRO text:REQ
    LOCAL hookText, reached, done
    .data
    hookText db text, 0
    reached  db 0
    .code
    pushfd
    cmp reached, 0
    jne done
    mov reached, 1
    pushad
    push OFFSET hookText
    call UhcTraceHook
    popad
done:
    popfd
ENDM
```

## Cfg reader

### src/config.h

```cpp
// uhc.cfg reader. The format is one key per line followed by whitespace-separated arguments;
// blank lines and lines starting with '#' are ignored. Keys are case-insensitive. The UHC
// patcher's syntax is accepted too, since it writes it: key=value, and "//" comments anywhere.
#pragma once

#include <map>
#include <optional>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

namespace uhc {

class Config {
 public:
  using Args = std::vector<std::string>;

  // Returns false if the file could not be read; an empty file is a valid, empty config.
  bool Load(const std::wstring& path);

  bool Has(const std::string& key) const;
  // Arguments of the first line with this key, or nullptr.
  const Args* First(const std::string& key) const;
  // Every line with this key, in file order.
  const std::vector<Args>* All(const std::string& key) const;
  // The first argument parsed as a decimal int; nullopt if the key is absent or malformed.
  std::optional<int> Int(const std::string& key) const;

  const std::map<std::string, std::vector<Args>>& entries() const { return entries_; }

  // For the options menu's switches (menuoptions.h), before any key applies: a line of a key with no arguments,
  // and the removal of every line of a key.
  void Add(const std::string& key);
  void Erase(const std::string& key);

 private:
  std::map<std::string, std::vector<Args>> entries_;  // keys are lowercase
};

}  // namespace uhc
```

### src/config.cpp

```cpp
#include "config.h"

#include <windows.h>

#include <cctype>
#include <charconv>

namespace uhc {
namespace {

std::string Lower(std::string s) {
  for (char& c : s) {
    c = static_cast<char>(std::tolower(static_cast<unsigned char>(c)));
  }
  return s;
}

bool IsSpace(char c) { return c == ' ' || c == '\t' || c == '\r'; }

// Where the line [pos, end) stops counting: at "//" (a comment in the UHC patcher's syntax), or at its end.
size_t CommentStart(const std::string& text, size_t pos, size_t end) {
  for (size_t i = pos; i + 1 < end; ++i) {
    if (text[i] == '/' && text[i + 1] == '/') {
      return i;
    }
  }
  return end;
}

}  // namespace

bool Config::Load(const std::wstring& path) {
  entries_.clear();
  HANDLE file = CreateFileW(path.c_str(), GENERIC_READ, FILE_SHARE_READ, nullptr, OPEN_EXISTING,
                            FILE_ATTRIBUTE_NORMAL, nullptr);
  if (file == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) {
    return false;
  }
  std::string text;
  LARGE_INTEGER size{};
  if (GetFileSizeEx(file, &size) && size.QuadPart > 0 && size.QuadPart < (1 << 24)) {
    text.resize(static_cast<size_t>(size.QuadPart));
    DWORD read = 0;
    if (!ReadFile(file, text.data(), static_cast<DWORD>(text.size()), &read, nullptr)) {
      read = 0;
    }
    text.resize(read);
  }
  CloseHandle(file);

  size_t pos = 0;
  while (pos < text.size()) {
    size_t end = text.find('\n', pos);
    if (end == std::string::npos) {
      end = text.size();
    }
    const size_t stop = CommentStart(text, pos, end);
    Args words;
    size_t i = pos;
    while (i < stop) {
      while (i < stop && IsSpace(text[i])) {
        ++i;
      }
      const size_t start = i;
      while (i < stop && !IsSpace(text[i])) {
        ++i;
      }
      if (i > start) {
        words.emplace_back(text, start, i - start);
      }
    }
    pos = end + 1;
    if (words.empty() || words.front().front() == '#') {
      continue;
    }
    std::string key = std::move(words.front());
    words.erase(words.begin());
    // The syntax the UHC patcher writes: key=value (also accept key = value).
    const size_t equals = key.find('=');
    if (equals != std::string::npos) {
      std::string value = key.substr(equals + 1);
      key.resize(equals);
      if (!value.empty()) {
        words.insert(words.begin(), std::move(value));
      }
    } else if (!words.empty() && words.front().front() == '=') {
      words.front().erase(0, 1);
      if (words.front().empty()) {
        words.erase(words.begin());
      }
    }
    if (!key.empty()) {
      entries_[Lower(key)].push_back(std::move(words));
    }
  }
  return true;
}

bool Config::Has(const std::string& key) const { return entries_.count(Lower(key)) != 0; }

const Config::Args* Config::First(const std::string& key) const {
  const auto* all = All(key);
  return all == nullptr ? nullptr : &all->front();
}

const std::vector<Config::Args>* Config::All(const std::string& key) const {
  const auto it = entries_.find(Lower(key));
  return it == entries_.end() ? nullptr : &it->second;
}

void Config::Add(const std::string& key) { entries_[Lower(key)].emplace_back(); }

void Config::Erase(const std::string& key) { entries_.erase(Lower(key)); }

std::optional<int> Config::Int(const std::string& key) const {
  const Args* args = First(key);
  if (args == nullptr || args->empty()) {
    return std::nullopt;
  }
  const std::string& s = args->front();
  int value = 0;
  const auto [end, ec] = std::from_chars(s.data(), s.data() + s.size(), value);
  if (ec != std::errc() || end != s.data() + s.size()) {
    return std::nullopt;
  }
  return value;
}

}  // namespace uhc
```

## Extra abstract unit types

### src/unittypes.h

```cpp
// Extra abstract unit types. The game registers a fixed list of abstract unittype names
// (FUN_0051A5DC); a <UnitType> in protoy.xml that is not on it is dropped. Features that read a
// marker type from data request it here, and the name is registered after the stock list on
// every database load, so protoy.xml and techtrees can use it like any built-in type.
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

namespace uhc {

// Registers `name` (copied) as an abstract unit type. On each database load `*id` receives its
// unittype ID (the existing one if the data already defines the name); it is -1 until then.
// Installs the registration hook on first use; false if the host bytes do not match.
bool RequestAbstractUnitType(const char* name, std::int32_t* id);

// Calls `callback(database)` on every database load, from the same hook: after the abstract types
// are registered and before the contents of the protos (their <ProtoAction> children among them)
// are read. Installs the hook on first use; false if the host bytes do not match.
bool OnDatabaseLoad(void (*callback)(char* database));

}  // namespace uhc
```

### src/unittypes.cpp

```cpp
#include "unittypes.h"

#include <string>
#include <vector>

#include "generated/sites.h"
#include "log.h"
#include "patch.h"

namespace {

struct Request {
  std::string name;
  std::int32_t* id;
};

std::vector<Request> g_requests;
std::vector<void (*)(char*)> g_loadCallbacks;
bool g_hooked = false;

}  // namespace

extern "C" {
void UhcAbstractUnitTypesHook();

// Called from the hook at the end of FUN_0051A5DC, after the stock abstract types are
// registered. The unittype table is at database+190h; its name count, [database+194h], is the
// next free ID (the stock code asserts IDs are dense). IDs below [database+19B0h] are protos.
// The per-proto unittype bitsets are sized later from [database+194h] - [database+19B0h]
// (0x5F769A), so they grow with every name added here.
void __stdcall UhcRegisterAbstractUnitTypes(char* database) {
  using NameBucket = int(__thiscall*)(void* table, const char* name);
  using FindName = bool(__thiscall*)(void* table, const char* name, int bucket, int* id);
  using AddName = void(__thiscall*)(void* table, const char* name, const int* id);
  const auto nameBucket = reinterpret_cast<NameBucket>(0x0058E685);
  const auto findName = reinterpret_cast<FindName>(0x0040295B);
  const auto addName = reinterpret_cast<AddName>(0x005E3549);

  void* table = database + 0x190;
  const auto count = reinterpret_cast<const std::int32_t*>(database + 0x194);
  const auto protoCount = *reinterpret_cast<const std::int32_t*>(database + 0x19B0);
  for (const Request& request : g_requests) {
    const char* name = request.name.c_str();
    int id = -1;
    const bool existed = findName(table, name, nameBucket(table, name), &id);
    if (!existed) {
      id = *count;
      addName(table, name, &id);
    }
    *request.id = id;
    uhc::Log(L"unit types: %hs -> %d (bit %d of %d)%ls", name, id, id - protoCount, *count - protoCount,
             existed ? L", already defined" : L", added");
  }
  for (void (*callback)(char*) : g_loadCallbacks) {
    callback(database);
  }
}
}

namespace {

bool InstallHook() {
  if (!g_hooked) {
    if (!uhc::VerifyAll("abstract unit types", {&uhc::sites::kAbstractTypesEnd})) {
      return false;
    }
    // Replaces pop edi / pop esi / mov al,1 / pop ebx at 0x51BA31; the hook re-executes them.
    if (!uhc::WriteJmp(0x0051BA31, reinterpret_cast<const void*>(&UhcAbstractUnitTypesHook), 5)) {
      return false;
    }
    g_hooked = true;
  }
  return true;
}

}  // namespace

namespace uhc {

bool RequestAbstractUnitType(const char* name, std::int32_t* id) {
  *id = -1;
  if (!InstallHook()) {
    return false;
  }
  g_requests.push_back({name, id});
  return true;
}

bool OnDatabaseLoad(void (*callback)(char* database)) {
  if (!InstallHook()) {
    return false;
  }
  g_loadCallbacks.push_back(callback);
  return true;
}

}  // namespace uhc
```

### src/hooks/unittype_hooks.asm

```asm
; Hook for the extra abstract unit types (unittypes.cpp). Entered through a jmp written over the
; stock code; leaves through an explicit jmp to a verified continuation.
.686
.model flat, c
option casemap :none

include common.inc

UhcRegisterAbstractUnitTypes PROTO STDCALL :DWORD

.data
epilogueRest    dd 0051BA36h    ; pop ecx / ret

.code

; Replaces 0x51BA31..0x51BA35 (pop edi / pop esi / mov al,1 / pop ebx), the epilogue of
; FUN_0051A5DC once the stock abstract types are registered. EDI = database.
UhcAbstractUnitTypesHook PROC
    TRACE_ONCE "abstract unit types (0x51BA31)"
    pushad
    push edi
    call UhcRegisterAbstractUnitTypes
    popad
    pop edi
    pop esi
    mov al, 1
    pop ebx
    jmp epilogueRest
UhcAbstractUnitTypesHook ENDP

END
```

## World tick

### src/worldtick.h

```cpp
// One wrapper around the world update FUN_00481A57 (thiscall(world, int, deltaMs), ret 8), shared
// by every key that needs a per-step tick (damageOverTime, overhealDecay): a single jmp at its
// entry, whichever of those keys are on.
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

namespace uhc {

// Called after each stock world update with the world and the update's deltaMs, in the order the
// keys added them (their kFeatures order, the same on every machine).
using WorldTick = void (*)(char* world, std::int32_t deltaMs);

// Writes the wrapper's jmp the first time (after checking the site's stock bytes) and adds tick.
bool AddWorldTick(WorldTick tick);

}  // namespace uhc
```

### src/worldtick.cpp

```cpp
#include "worldtick.h"

#include <cstddef>

#include "generated/sites.h"
#include "log.h"
#include "patch.h"

namespace {

constexpr std::size_t kMaxTicks = 12;

using WorldUpdateFn = bool(__fastcall*)(char* world, void* unused, std::int32_t a, std::int32_t deltaMs);

uhc::WorldTick g_ticks[kMaxTicks] = {};
std::size_t g_tickCount = 0;
bool g_installed = false;

}  // namespace

extern "C" {
void UhcWorldUpdateStock();  // hooks/worldtick_hooks.asm: the stolen prologue, then the stock body
void UhcWorldUpdateHook();

// The stock world update, then every tick in order. Same frame as the stock thiscall: ECX = world,
// the two stack arguments, ret 8.
bool __fastcall UhcWorldUpdate(char* world, void*, std::int32_t a, std::int32_t deltaMs) {
  const bool result = reinterpret_cast<WorldUpdateFn>(&UhcWorldUpdateStock)(world, nullptr, a, deltaMs);
  for (std::size_t i = 0; i < g_tickCount; ++i) {
    g_ticks[i](world, deltaMs);
  }
  return result;
}

void* g_uhcWorldUpdate = reinterpret_cast<void*>(&UhcWorldUpdate);
}

namespace uhc {

bool AddWorldTick(WorldTick tick) {
  if (g_tickCount == kMaxTicks) {
    Log(L"world update: more than %u ticks", static_cast<unsigned>(kMaxTicks));
    return false;
  }
  if (!g_installed) {
    // sub esp,0Ch / push ebx / push ebp at 0x481A57; the trampoline re-executes them.
    if (!VerifyAll("world update", {&sites::kDotWorldUpdateEntry}) ||
        !WriteJmp(sites::kDotWorldUpdateEntry.va, reinterpret_cast<const void*>(&UhcWorldUpdateHook), 5)) {
      return false;
    }
    g_installed = true;
  }
  g_ticks[g_tickCount++] = tick;
  return true;
}

}  // namespace uhc
```

### src/hooks/worldtick_hooks.asm

```asm
; The world update wrapper's entry and trampoline (worldtick.cpp), shared by every key that needs a
; per-step tick. Entered through a jmp written over the stock code at 0x481A57.
.686
.model flat, c
option casemap :none

include common.inc

EXTERNDEF g_uhcWorldUpdate:DWORD        ; bool fastcall(world, -, the 2 stock arguments)

.data
worldUpdateBody     dd 00481A5Ch    ; push esi / mov esi,ecx, after the stolen prologue

.code

; Replaces sub esp,0Ch / push ebx / push ebp (5 bytes) at 0x481A57, the entry of the world update
; FUN_00481A57 (thiscall(world, int, deltaMs), ret 8).
; The frame is unchanged, so the fastcall wrapper receives it as its own: ECX = world, the stack
; arguments in place.
UhcWorldUpdateHook PROC
    TRACE_ONCE "world update (0x481A57)"
    jmp g_uhcWorldUpdate
UhcWorldUpdateHook ENDP

; The stock world update for the wrapper: the stolen prologue, then the rest of the function.
UhcWorldUpdateStock PROC
    sub esp, 0Ch
    push ebx
    push ebp
    jmp worldUpdateBody
UhcWorldUpdateStock ENDP

END
```

## Action tags

### src/actiontags.h

```cpp
// One hook on the unknown-tag fall-through of the action parser FUN_005F98A0 (0x5FA11E), shared by
// every key that reads extra action tags (damageOverTime, spreadAnyAccuracy): a single jmp,
// whichever of those keys are on. The parser reads both a protoy.xml <ProtoAction> and a .tactics
// file's <action>; stock ignores the tags it does not know.
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

namespace uhc {

enum class ActionSource {
  kProtoAction,  // a <ProtoAction> of protoy.xml (the parser called from 0x5FA20E)
  kTactics,      // an <action> of a .tactics file (called from 0x5FA61E, FUN_005FA5B3)
};

// One child of an action that the stock parser does not know.
struct ActionTag {
  const wchar_t* name;  // the child's tag name, as written (compare case-insensitively)
  const wchar_t* text;  // its text; may be null or empty
  const char* proto;    // the proto whose action set holds the action: [proto+4] ID, [proto+10h] name
  const char* action;   // the action: [action+4] name index, -1 until the action's <Name> is read
  ActionSource source;
};

// Called for each such child while the data loads, in file order, by the handlers in the order the
// keys added them (their kFeatures order).
using ActionTagHandler = void (*)(const ActionTag& tag);

// Writes the hook the first time (after checking the sites' stock bytes) and adds handler.
bool AddActionTagHandler(ActionTagHandler handler);

}  // namespace uhc
```

### src/actiontags.cpp

```cpp
#include "actiontags.h"

#include <cstddef>

#include "generated/sites.h"
#include "log.h"
#include "patch.h"

namespace {

constexpr std::size_t kMaxHandlers = 12;
// Return address of the parser's call by the <ProtoAction> handler; the hook passes only that one
// and 0x5FA623, the tactics <action> creator's (docs/engine-symbols.md, "Damage and poison").
constexpr std::uint32_t kFromProtoAction = 0x005FA213;

uhc::ActionTagHandler g_handlers[kMaxHandlers] = {};
std::size_t g_handlerCount = 0;
bool g_installed = false;

}  // namespace

extern "C" {
void UhcActionTagHook();  // hooks/actiontag_hooks.asm

// From the hook, for each child of an action the stock parser does not know. tag is the address of
// the child's tag-name BString (its UTF-16 text is *tag); the child's text is at [node+14h].
void __stdcall UhcActionTag(const wchar_t* const* tag, const char* node, const char* proto, const char* action,
                            std::uint32_t returnAddress) {
  if (tag == nullptr || *tag == nullptr || node == nullptr || proto == nullptr || action == nullptr) {
    return;
  }
  const uhc::ActionTag child{*tag, *reinterpret_cast<const wchar_t* const*>(node + 0x14), proto, action,
                             returnAddress == kFromProtoAction ? uhc::ActionSource::kProtoAction
                                                               : uhc::ActionSource::kTactics};
  for (std::size_t i = 0; i < g_handlerCount; ++i) {
    g_handlers[i](child);
  }
}
}

namespace uhc {

bool AddActionTagHandler(ActionTagHandler handler) {
  if (g_handlerCount == kMaxHandlers) {
    Log(L"action tags: more than %u handlers", static_cast<unsigned>(kMaxHandlers));
    return false;
  }
  if (!g_installed) {
    // test eax,eax / je 5FD4EDh at 0x5FA11E; the hook re-executes them. Its two callers' return
    // addresses are checked as well, since the hook tells them apart by those.
    if (!VerifyAll("action tags", {&sites::kDotProtoActionTag, &sites::kDotProtoActionCall,
                                   &sites::kTacticsActionCall}) ||
        !WriteJmp(0x005FA11E, reinterpret_cast<const void*>(&UhcActionTagHook), 8)) {
      return false;
    }
    g_installed = true;
  }
  g_handlers[g_handlerCount++] = handler;
  return true;
}

}  // namespace uhc
```

### src/hooks/actiontag_hooks.asm

```asm
; The action tag hook (actiontags.cpp), shared by every key that reads extra action tags
; (damageOverTime, spreadAnyAccuracy). Entered through a jmp written over the stock code at 0x5FA11E;
; it leaves through an explicit jmp to a verified continuation (docs/engine-symbols.md, "Damage and
; poison").
.686
.model flat, c
option casemap :none

include common.inc

UhcActionTag PROTO STDCALL :DWORD, :DWORD, :DWORD, :DWORD, :DWORD

.data
lastTagHandler      dd 005FD4EDh    ; stock je target: the "ModifyExclusive" handler
nextChild           dd 005FA126h    ; mov eax,[esp+1Ch]: the loop's next child

.code

; Replaces test eax,eax / je 5FD4EDh at 0x5FA11E in FUN_005F98A0 (thiscall(actionSet, proto, action,
; node)), after its last tag compare ("ModifyExclusive"). Reached only by that tag and by tags the
; stock parser does not know.
; ESI = the address of the child's tag-name BString (its UTF-16 text is [esi]), EDI = the child node;
; the arguments are at [esp+0C4h] (proto) and [esp+0C8h] (action), the return address at [esp+0C0h]:
; 0x5FA213 for a protoy.xml <ProtoAction>, 0x5FA623 for a .tactics <action>. Unknown tags of either go
; to the C++ dispatcher. EAX and the flags are free: the next-child code overwrites them
; (mov eax,[esp+1Ch] / ... / cmp eax,[ecx+0Ch]).
UhcActionTagHook PROC
    test eax, eax                   ; stolen 0x5FA11E
    jne unknownTag
    jmp lastTagHandler              ; stolen 0x5FA120: je 5FD4EDh
unknownTag:
    mov eax, dword ptr [esp+0C0h]
    cmp eax, 005FA213h              ; the <ProtoAction> handler's call
    je dispatch
    cmp eax, 005FA623h              ; the tactics <action> creator's call
    jne next
dispatch:
    TRACE_ONCE "action tag (0x5FA11E)"
    pushad
    push dword ptr [esp+20h+0C0h]       ; return address: the action's source
    push dword ptr [esp+24h+0C8h]       ; action
    push dword ptr [esp+28h+0C4h]       ; proto
    push edi                            ; child node
    push esi                            ; tag-name BString address
    call UhcActionTag
    popad
next:
    jmp nextChild
UhcActionTagHook ENDP

END
```

## Transform calls

### src/transformcalls.h

```cpp
// The seven calls of the in-place transform 0x9C754C, shared by every key that acts around it. A key
// never writes one of those calls: it adds an observer here, and calls TransformUnit instead of 0x9C754C.
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

namespace uhc {

// `before` runs ahead of the transform and returns what `after` gets, for the state the transform
// erases, like the unit's modifiers.
struct TransformObserver {
  float (*before)(const char* unit);
  void (*after)(char* unit, float saved);
};

// Writes the calls the first time, after checking the stock bytes.
bool AddTransformObserver(const TransformObserver& observer);

// Takes the call of the TransformUnit effect's apply, 0x7FBC79: `wrapper` runs instead, and has to call
// TransformUnit itself. One key at most.
using TransformEffectWrapper = void (*)(char* unit, std::int32_t proto);
bool SetTransformEffectWrapper(TransformEffectWrapper wrapper);

// Keys that read an attribute of a TransformUnit effect. The first call takes the effect's SetData and Apply
// slots, after checking them. `read` runs when the effect's XML was read and is good; `before` and `after`
// run around each apply. Any of them may be null.
struct TransformEffectReader {
  void (*read)(void* effect, const void* node);
  void (*before)(void* effect);
  void (*after)(void* effect);
};
bool AddTransformEffectReader(const TransformEffectReader& reader);

// 0x9C754C with the observers around it. Every key's own transform goes through here.
std::int32_t TransformUnit(char* unit, std::int32_t proto);

}  // namespace uhc
```

### src/transformcalls.cpp

```cpp
#include "transformcalls.h"

#include <cstddef>

#include "generated/sites.h"
#include "log.h"
#include "patch.h"

namespace {

constexpr std::size_t kMaxObservers = 4;
constexpr std::size_t kMaxReaders = 4;
constexpr std::uint32_t kEffectCall = 0x007FBC79;  // the TransformUnit effect's apply
constexpr std::uint32_t kOtherCalls[] = {0x007FBEF2, 0x008C2D08, 0x008EB78A, 0x008F05F5, 0x009BB6D5, 0x009C7C16};

using TransformFn = std::int32_t(__thiscall*)(char* unit, std::int32_t proto);
using SetDataFn = bool(__thiscall*)(void* effect, const void* node);
using ApplyFn = void(__thiscall*)(void* effect, void* a, void* b, void* c, void* d);
const auto kTransform = reinterpret_cast<TransformFn>(0x009C754C);

uhc::TransformObserver g_observers[kMaxObservers] = {};
std::size_t g_count = 0;
uhc::TransformEffectWrapper g_effectWrapper = nullptr;
bool g_installed = false;
uhc::TransformEffectReader g_readers[kMaxReaders] = {};
std::size_t g_readerCount = 0;
SetDataFn g_setData = nullptr;
ApplyFn g_apply = nullptr;

bool __fastcall EffectSetData(void* effect, void*, const void* node) {
  const bool ok = g_setData(effect, node);
  if (ok && node != nullptr) {
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < g_readerCount; ++i) {
      if (g_readers[i].read != nullptr) {
        g_readers[i].read(effect, node);
      }
    }
  }
  return ok;
}

void __fastcall EffectApply(void* effect, void*, void* a, void* b, void* c, void* d) {
  for (std::size_t i = 0; i < g_readerCount; ++i) {
    if (g_readers[i].before != nullptr) {
      g_readers[i].before(effect);
    }
  }
  g_apply(effect, a, b, c, d);
  for (std::size_t i = g_readerCount; i > 0; --i) {
    if (g_readers[i - 1].after != nullptr) {
      g_readers[i - 1].after(effect);
    }
  }
}

std::int32_t __fastcall OtherCall(char* unit, void*, std::int32_t proto) { return uhc::TransformUnit(unit, proto); }

std::int32_t __fastcall EffectCall(char* unit, void*, std::int32_t proto) {
  if (g_effectWrapper == nullptr) {
    return uhc::TransformUnit(unit, proto);
  }
  g_effectWrapper(unit, proto);
  return 0;
}

bool Install() {
  if (g_installed) {
    return true;
  }
  if (!uhc::VerifyAll("transform calls", {&uhc::sites::kTransformCall1, &uhc::sites::kTransformCall2,
                                          &uhc::sites::kTransformCall3, &uhc::sites::kTransformCall4,
                                          &uhc::sites::kTransformCall5, &uhc::sites::kTransformCall6,
                                          &uhc::sites::kTransformCall7}) ||
      !uhc::WriteCall(kEffectCall, reinterpret_cast<const void*>(&EffectCall))) {
    return false;
  }
  for (const std::uint32_t call : kOtherCalls) {
    if (!uhc::WriteCall(call, reinterpret_cast<const void*>(&OtherCall))) {
      return false;
    }
  }
  g_installed = true;
  return true;
}

}  // namespace

namespace uhc {

bool AddTransformEffectReader(const TransformEffectReader& reader) {
  if (g_readerCount == kMaxReaders) {
    Log(L"transform effect: no room for another reader");
    return false;
  }
  if (g_readerCount == 0) {
    if (!VerifyAll("transform effect", {&sites::kTransformEffectSetDataSlot, &sites::kTransformEffectApplySlot})) {
      return false;
    }
    g_setData = *reinterpret_cast<SetDataFn*>(sites::kTransformEffectSetDataSlot.va);
    g_apply = *reinterpret_cast<ApplyFn*>(sites::kTransformEffectApplySlot.va);
    if (!WriteDword(sites::kTransformEffectSetDataSlot.va, reinterpret_cast<std::uintptr_t>(&EffectSetData)) ||
        !WriteDword(sites::kTransformEffectApplySlot.va, reinterpret_cast<std::uintptr_t>(&EffectApply))) {
      return false;
    }
  }
  g_readers[g_readerCount++] = reader;
  return true;
}

bool AddTransformObserver(const TransformObserver& observer) {
  if (g_count == kMaxObservers) {
    Log(L"transform calls: no room for another observer");
    return false;
  }
  if (!Install()) {
    return false;
  }
  g_observers[g_count++] = observer;
  return true;
}

bool SetTransformEffectWrapper(TransformEffectWrapper wrapper) {
  if (g_effectWrapper != nullptr) {
    Log(L"transform calls: the effect's call is taken");
    return false;
  }
  if (!Install()) {
    return false;
  }
  g_effectWrapper = wrapper;
  return true;
}

std::int32_t TransformUnit(char* unit, std::int32_t proto) {
  float saved[kMaxObservers] = {};
  for (std::size_t i = 0; i < g_count; ++i) {
    saved[i] = g_observers[i].before(unit);
  }
  const std::int32_t result = kTransform(unit, proto);
  for (std::size_t i = 0; i < g_count; ++i) {
    g_observers[i].after(unit, saved[i]);
  }
  return result;
}

}  // namespace uhc
```

## Proto tags

### src/prototags.h

```cpp
// New tags inside a proto's <Unit>: one hook on the tail of the tag table's builder, shared by every
// key that adds a tag.
#pragma once

namespace uhc {

// The stock handler convention. `node` is the tag's XML node (its text, a BString, is at +14h), `proto`
// the base proto being read. Return true; false stops reading that proto.
using ProtoTagHandler = bool(__cdecl*)(void* context, const char* node, char* proto);

// Registers <name> (static text). Call it from a feature's apply function: the table is built later,
// during the game's startup. Writes the hook the first time, after checking the stock bytes.
bool AddProtoTagHandler(const char* name, ProtoTagHandler handler);

}  // namespace uhc
```

### src/prototags.cpp

```cpp
#include "prototags.h"

#include <cstddef>

#include "generated/sites.h"
#include "log.h"
#include "patch.h"

extern "C" void UhcProtoTagsHook();  // hooks/prototag_hooks.asm

namespace {

constexpr std::size_t kMaxTags = 32;
constexpr std::uint32_t kTableTail = 0x0051DB0F;  // pop edi / mov al,1 / pop esi / pop ecx (5 B)

using RegisterFn = void(__thiscall*)(void* table, const char* name, uhc::ProtoTagHandler* handler);
const auto kRegister = reinterpret_cast<RegisterFn>(0x0059B738);

struct Tag {
  const char* name;
  uhc::ProtoTagHandler handler;
};

Tag g_tags[kMaxTags] = {};
std::size_t g_count = 0;
bool g_installed = false;

}  // namespace

// From the hook, with the table the builder just filled.
extern "C" void __stdcall UhcProtoTagsRegister(void* table) {
  for (std::size_t i = 0; i < g_count; ++i) {
    uhc::ProtoTagHandler handler = g_tags[i].handler;
    kRegister(table, g_tags[i].name, &handler);
  }
}

namespace uhc {

bool AddProtoTagHandler(const char* name, ProtoTagHandler handler) {
  if (g_count == kMaxTags) {
    Log(L"proto tags: no room for %hs", name);
    return false;
  }
  if (!g_installed) {
    if (!VerifyAll("proto tags", {&sites::kProtoTagsTail}) ||
        !WriteJmp(kTableTail, reinterpret_cast<const void*>(&UhcProtoTagsHook), 5)) {
      return false;
    }
    g_installed = true;
  }
  g_tags[g_count++] = {name, handler};
  return true;
}

}  // namespace uhc
```

### src/hooks/prototag_hooks.asm

```asm
; The shared hook for new proto tags (src/prototags.h). Entered through a jmp written over the stock
; code; it leaves through an explicit jmp to a verified continuation.
.686
.model flat, c
option casemap :none

include common.inc

UhcProtoTagsRegister PROTO STDCALL table:DWORD

.data
tableBuilderRet dd 0051DB14h    ; ret of FUN_0051CE1D

.code

; Replaces 0x51DB0F..0x51DB13 (pop edi / mov al,1 / pop esi / pop ecx), the tail of the tag table's
; builder, after its last registration. EDI = the builder's object: the protos' table is at +0Ch
; (0x51CE27), and ESI by now is the second table, at +0C2Ch (0x51D807). The hook re-executes those
; instructions after the helper.
UhcProtoTagsHook PROC
    TRACE_ONCE "proto tags (0x51DB0F)"
    lea eax, [edi+0Ch]
    invoke UhcProtoTagsRegister, eax
    pop edi
    mov al, 1
    pop esi
    pop ecx
    jmp tableBuilderRet
UhcProtoTagsHook ENDP

END
```

## Civ tags

### src/civtags.h

```cpp
// Tags of a <civ> in civs.xml that stock does not know: one hook on the parser's unknown-tag path,
// shared by every key that reads one (civFirstTC's <firstTC>, sharedBuildLimit's <SettlerProtoName>).
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

namespace uhc {

// `civ` is the civ object being filled (+8 its ID), `text` the element's text (may be null).
using CivTagHandler = void (*)(const char* civ, std::int32_t civId, const wchar_t* text);

// Makes a <civ> child element named `name` (case ignored) reach `handler`. Writes the hook the
// first time, after checking the stock bytes. The parse runs after UHCMain, so every tag is seen.
bool AddCivTagHandler(const char* name, CivTagHandler handler);

}  // namespace uhc
```

### src/civtags.cpp

```cpp
#include "civtags.h"

#include <cstddef>
#include <cwchar>

#include "generated/sites.h"
#include "log.h"
#include "patch.h"

extern "C" void UhcCivTagsHook();  // hooks/civfirsttc_hooks.asm

namespace {

constexpr std::size_t kMaxTags = 8;
constexpr std::uint32_t kUnknownTag = 0x0052BB07;  // xor edi,edi / cmp dword ptr [ebx+80h],edi (8 B)

struct Tag {
  const char* name;
  uhc::CivTagHandler handler;
};

Tag g_tags[kMaxTags] = {};
std::size_t g_count = 0;
bool g_installed = false;

template <typename T>
T Field(const char* base, std::uint32_t offset) {
  return *reinterpret_cast<const T*>(base + offset);
}

// name (narrow) == tag (wide), case ignored.
bool SameName(const char* name, const wchar_t* tag) {
  for (;; ++name, ++tag) {
    const wchar_t a = static_cast<wchar_t>(*name >= 'A' && *name <= 'Z' ? *name + 32 : *name);
    const wchar_t b = *tag >= L'A' && *tag <= L'Z' ? *tag + 32 : *tag;
    if (a != b) {
      return false;
    }
    if (a == 0) {
      return true;
    }
  }
}

}  // namespace

// From the hook: true if the node's name is a registered tag (then it is consumed).
extern "C" bool __stdcall UhcCivTagDispatch(const char* node, const char* civ) {
  // The element name, as the parser's compare chain reads it: [[node+4]+18h][[node+18h]].
  const std::int32_t index = Field<std::int32_t>(node, 0x18);
  const char* names = Field<const char*>(node, 0x04);
  if (index < 0 || names == nullptr || index >= Field<std::int32_t>(names, 0x1C)) {
    return false;
  }
  const wchar_t* tag = Field<const wchar_t* const*>(names, 0x18)[index];
  if (tag == nullptr) {
    return false;
  }
  for (std::size_t i = 0; i < g_count; ++i) {
    if (!SameName(g_tags[i].name, tag)) {
      continue;
    }
    const std::int32_t civId = civ == nullptr ? -1 : Field<std::int32_t>(civ, 0x08);
    if (civId >= 0 && civId < 0x10000) {
      g_tags[i].handler(civ, civId, Field<const wchar_t*>(node, 0x14));
    }
    return true;
  }
  return false;
}

namespace uhc {

bool AddCivTagHandler(const char* name, CivTagHandler handler) {
  if (g_count == kMaxTags) {
    Log(L"civ tags: no room for %hs", name);
    return false;
  }
  if (!g_installed) {
    if (!VerifyAll("civ tags", {&sites::kCivTagUnknown, &sites::kCivTagNextNode}) ||
        !WriteJmp(kUnknownTag, reinterpret_cast<const void*>(&UhcCivTagsHook), 8)) {
      return false;
    }
    g_installed = true;
  }
  g_tags[g_count++] = {name, handler};
  return true;
}

}  // namespace uhc
```

### src/hooks/civfirsttc_hooks.asm

```asm
; Hooks for civFirstTC. Entered through a jmp written over the stock code; each leaves through an
; explicit jmp to a verified continuation (docs/engine-symbols.md, "Civ first town center"). The
; FirstTC test is in startingtc_hooks.asm (UhcStartingTcProtoHook), shared with startingTC, and the
; map placement wrapper is C++ (UhcRmPlaceObjectDef in features/civfirsttc.cpp).
.686
.model flat, c
option casemap :none

include common.inc

UhcCivTagDispatch PROTO STDCALL node:DWORD, civ:DWORD  ; civtags.cpp; true: a registered tag, consumed
UhcCivFirstTcTownProto PROTO STDCALL player:DWORD      ; the proto for the town units, or -1
UhcCivFirstTcColonyFlag PROTO STDCALL unit:DWORD       ; true: this unit counts as StartingColonyBuilding

.data
civTagResume    dd 0052BB0Fh    ; jle 52B43Dh / jmp 52C066h: the stock unknown-tag path, on the flags
civTagNext      dd 0052B43Dh    ; the next child of the <civ> node
colonyFlagResume dd 006A2136h   ; and eax,1 / mov [esp+74h],eax: the rest of the test of flag 136
townQueryResume dd 00679ACEh    ; push ebp / push esi / mov esi,[edi+80h]: the query of proto EBX

.code

; Replaces 0x52BB07..0x52BB0E (xor edi,edi / cmp dword ptr [ebx+80h],edi) in the civs.xml
; parser FUN_0052A590: a child tag of <civ> that matched none of the stock names. ESI = the node,
; EBX = database, [esp+10h] = the civ. UhcCivTagDispatch (civtags.cpp, shared by civFirstTC and
; sharedBuildLimit) reads a registered tag and the hook goes to the next child, as every stock
; handler does; any other tag runs the stock path (the resource names, then a silent skip).
; 0x52B43D reads only the stack.
UhcCivTagsHook PROC
    TRACE_ONCE "civ tags civs.xml (0x52BB07)"
    pushad
    push dword ptr [esp+30h]        ; the civ: [esp+10h] at entry, +20h for pushad
    push esi                        ; the node
    call UhcCivTagDispatch
    mov dword ptr [esp+1Ch], eax    ; popad's EAX slot
    popad
    test al, al
    jne nextChild
    xor edi, edi                    ; stolen 0x52BB07
    cmp dword ptr [ebx+80h], edi    ; stolen 0x52BB09: the flags the jle at 0x52BB0F reads
    jmp civTagResume
nextChild:
    jmp civTagNext
UhcCivTagsHook ENDP

; Replaces 0x679AC8..0x679ACD (mov ebx,[ecx+5E08h]) in FUN_0049039A, which gives the civ's
; <townstartingunit> units to the first built unit of proto EBX (stock: TownCenter) when a colony
; starts at it. EDI = player. EAX, ECX and EDX are written before they are read after 0x679ACE.
; UhcCivFirstTcTownProto returns the civ's <firstTC> proto when the player's FirstTC is such a
; building with a colony; otherwise EBX stays TownCenter.
UhcCivFirstTcTownHook PROC
    TRACE_ONCE "civFirstTC town units (0x679AC8)"
    mov ebx, dword ptr [ecx+5E08h]  ; stolen 0x679AC8: TownCenter
    push edi
    call UhcCivFirstTcTownProto
    test eax, eax
    jl done
    mov ebx, eax
done:
    jmp townQueryResume
UhcCivFirstTcTownHook ENDP

; Replaces 0x6A2130..0x6A2135 with the key firstTC, at the creation of a unit, ESI: the test of flag 136 of its
; proto, StartingColonyBuilding. A civ's <firstTC> building counts as having it, without writing the proto,
; which every civ shares. AL is what `and eax,1` reads; EAX's other bytes do not matter there.
UhcCivFirstTcColonyHook PROC
    TRACE_ONCE "firstTC colony flag (0x6A2130)"
    mov edx, dword ptr [eax+50h]    ; stolen 0x6A2130
    mov al, byte ptr [edx+11h]      ; stolen 0x6A2133
    test al, 1
    jne done
    push eax
    push ecx
    push edx
    push esi
    call UhcCivFirstTcColonyFlag
    test al, al
    pop edx
    pop ecx
    pop eax
    je done
    or al, 1
done:
    jmp colonyFlagResume
UhcCivFirstTcColonyHook ENDP

END
```

## Tech subtypes

### src/techsubtypes.h

```cpp
// New subtypes of the tech effect type="Data", on ProtoUnit targets: one hook on the name lookup and
// one on each of the two subtype switches, shared by every key that adds a subtype.
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

namespace uhc {

// `proto` is the player's copy, `base` the proto it was cloned from. `relativity` and `amount` are the
// effect's. `unapply` is set when the effect is removed.
using TechSubtypeHandler = void (*)(char* proto, const char* base, float amount, std::uint8_t relativity,
                                    bool unapply);

// Makes subtype="<name>" (static text; case ignored) reach `handler`. Writes the hooks the first time,
// after checking the stock bytes.
bool AddTechSubtype(const char* name, TechSubtypeHandler handler);

// The same for a subtype on a proto's action: the effect takes action="<Action>" or allactions="1", as
// stock's TrackRating and Damage do. `action` is the player's copy, once for each action the effect
// names, and `baseAction` the action of that name in the base proto, or nullptr if it has none. `proto`
// is the player's copy of the proto ([proto+4] its ID). A proto without the action is left alone.
using TechActionSubtypeHandler = void (*)(char* action, const char* baseAction, const char* proto, float amount,
                                          std::uint8_t relativity, bool unapply);
bool AddTechActionSubtype(const char* name, TechActionSubtypeHandler handler);

// The same for a subtype that names an abstract unit type: the effect takes unittype="<Type>", which must
// exist when the techs are read. `type` is its ID in the unit type table. An unknown name, or a proto's,
// is logged and the effect dropped.
using TechTypeSubtypeHandler = void (*)(char* proto, const char* base, std::int32_t type, bool unapply);
bool AddTechTypeSubtype(const char* name, TechTypeSubtypeHandler handler);

// Called for a Data subtype that neither stock nor a key knows, while the data loads, for dataLint. Writes the hooks too.
using UnknownTechSubtype = void (*)(const char* name);
bool OnUnknownTechSubtype(UnknownTechSubtype callback);

}  // namespace uhc
```

### src/techsubtypes.cpp

```cpp
#include "techsubtypes.h"

#include <cstddef>
#include <cstring>

#include "generated/sites.h"
#include "log.h"
#include "patch.h"

extern "C" {
void UhcTechSubtypeAttrHook();     // hooks/techsubtype_hooks.asm
void UhcTechSubtypeLookupHook();
void UhcTechSubtypeLookupStock();
void UhcTechSubtypeApplyHook();
void UhcTechSubtypeUnapplyHook();
char* g_uhcSubtypeEffect = nullptr;       // the effect whose subtype is being read, and its XML node
const char* g_uhcSubtypeNode = nullptr;
}

namespace {

constexpr std::size_t kMaxSubtypes = 32;
constexpr std::int32_t kFirstId = 0x100;  // above every stock subtype; the stock switches stop at 63h
constexpr std::uint32_t kNameLookupHead = 0x0061FF5D;  // the lookup
constexpr std::uint32_t kAttributeCall = 0x006206B2;   // setData's read of the attribute subtype
constexpr std::uint32_t kOtherCaller = 0x00621C2E;     // the lookup's other caller, which tests for one stock ID
constexpr std::uint32_t kApplySwitch = 0x008F978D;     // cmp eax,63h / ja (9 B)
constexpr std::uint32_t kUnapplySwitch = 0x008FB051;   // cmp eax,5Eh / ja (9 B)
constexpr std::size_t kEffectAction = 0x10;            // BDataEffect: the action's ID, -1 for allactions
constexpr std::size_t kEffectFlags = 9;                // bit 40h: allactions="1", read before the subtype
constexpr std::uint8_t kEffectAfterLoad = 0x20;         // in those flags
constexpr std::size_t kProtoActions = 0x44;            // the action set
constexpr std::size_t kActionList = 0x1C;              // in the set: the array of actions, and its count at +20h
constexpr std::size_t kActionId = 4;                   // in an action
constexpr std::size_t kTypeNames = 0x190;              // in the database: the unit type name table
constexpr std::size_t kFirstAbstract = 0x19B0;         // in the database: the first abstract type's ID

using NameLookupFn = std::int32_t(__thiscall*)(void* db, const char* name);
const auto kNameLookup = reinterpret_cast<NameLookupFn>(&UhcTechSubtypeLookupStock);
using XmlAttributeFn = bool(__thiscall*)(const void* node, const char* name, const void** out);
const auto kXmlAttribute = reinterpret_cast<XmlAttributeFn>(0x00598F37);
using ActionIdByNameFn = std::int32_t(__thiscall*)(void* db, const void* attribute);
const auto kActionIdByName = reinterpret_cast<ActionIdByNameFn>(0x005E0C64);
using ActionByIdFn = char*(__thiscall*)(const void* set, std::int32_t id);
const auto kActionById = reinterpret_cast<ActionByIdFn>(0x0048D22E);
using XmlTextFn = const char*(__thiscall*)(const void* text);
const auto kXmlText = reinterpret_cast<XmlTextFn>(0x00402733);
using NameBucketFn = std::int32_t(__thiscall*)(const void* table, const char* name);
const auto kNameBucket = reinterpret_cast<NameBucketFn>(0x0058E685);
using NameFindFn = bool(__thiscall*)(const void* table, const char* name, std::int32_t bucket, std::int32_t* id);
const auto kNameFind = reinterpret_cast<NameFindFn>(0x0040295B);

struct Subtype {
  const char* name;
  uhc::TechSubtypeHandler handler;              // on a proto
  uhc::TechActionSubtypeHandler actionHandler;  // or on its actions
  uhc::TechTypeSubtypeHandler typeHandler;      // or with a unit type
};

Subtype g_subtypes[kMaxSubtypes] = {};
std::size_t g_count = 0;
bool g_installed = false;
uhc::UnknownTechSubtype g_unknown = nullptr;

// What stock does in setData for TrackRating: only the subtypes it knows read the attribute.
bool ReadAction(void* db, const char* name, char* effect, const char* node) {
  auto* action = reinterpret_cast<std::int32_t*>(effect + kEffectAction);
  if ((static_cast<std::uint8_t>(effect[kEffectFlags]) & 0x40) != 0) {
    *action = -1;
    return true;
  }
  const void* attribute = nullptr;
  if (!kXmlAttribute(node, "action", &attribute) || attribute == nullptr) {
    uhc::Log(L"tech subtypes: a %hs effect names no action and has no allactions='1'; effect dropped", name);
    return false;
  }
  *action = kActionIdByName(db, attribute);
  if (*action == -1) {
    uhc::Log(L"tech subtypes: a %hs effect names an unknown action; effect dropped", name);
    return false;
  }
  return true;
}

// The attribute unittype, into the field the action's ID uses.
bool ReadType(void* db, const char* name, char* effect, const char* node) {
  const void* attribute = nullptr;
  if (!kXmlAttribute(node, "unittype", &attribute) || attribute == nullptr) {
    uhc::Log(L"tech subtypes: a %hs effect names no unittype; effect dropped", name);
    return false;
  }
  const char* text = kXmlText(attribute);
  const char* table = static_cast<const char*>(db) + kTypeNames;
  std::int32_t id = -1;
  if (text == nullptr || !kNameFind(table, text, kNameBucket(table, text), &id) || id < 0) {
    uhc::Log(L"tech subtypes: a %hs effect names the unknown unit type %hs; effect dropped", name,
             text == nullptr ? "" : text);
    return false;
  }
  if (id < *reinterpret_cast<const std::int32_t*>(static_cast<const char*>(db) + kFirstAbstract)) {
    uhc::Log(L"tech subtypes: a %hs effect names %hs, a proto and not an abstract type; effect dropped", name, text);
    return false;
  }
  *reinterpret_cast<std::int32_t*>(effect + kEffectAction) = id;
  // A proto's types are not in a saved game: the pass after a load, 0x94DDEA, runs the effects with this flag again.
  effect[kEffectFlags] = static_cast<char>(effect[kEffectFlags] | kEffectAfterLoad);
  return true;
}

bool RunOnActions(const Subtype& subtype, char* proto, const char* base, float amount, std::uint8_t relativity,
                  bool unapply, std::int32_t actionId, bool allActions) {
  const char* set = *reinterpret_cast<char* const*>(proto + kProtoActions);
  const char* baseSet = *reinterpret_cast<char* const*>(base + kProtoActions);
  if (set == nullptr || baseSet == nullptr) {
    return false;
  }
  if (!allActions) {
    if (char* action = kActionById(set, actionId)) {
      subtype.actionHandler(action, kActionById(baseSet, actionId), proto, amount, relativity, unapply);
    }
    return true;
  }
  char* const* actions = *reinterpret_cast<char* const* const*>(set + kActionList);
  const std::int32_t count = *reinterpret_cast<const std::int32_t*>(set + kActionList + 4);
  for (std::int32_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
    const std::int32_t id = *reinterpret_cast<const std::int32_t*>(actions[i] + kActionId);
    subtype.actionHandler(actions[i], kActionById(baseSet, id), proto, amount, relativity, unapply);
  }
  return true;
}

bool Install() {
  if (!g_installed) {
    if (!uhc::VerifyAll("tech subtypes", {&uhc::sites::kDataSubtypeLookupHead, &uhc::sites::kDataSubtypeAttrCall,
                                          &uhc::sites::kDataApplySwitch, &uhc::sites::kDataUnapplySwitch,
                                          &uhc::sites::kDataActionAttribute, &uhc::sites::kDataAllActionsApply,
                                          &uhc::sites::kDataAllActionsUnapply}) ||
        !uhc::WriteJmp(kNameLookupHead, reinterpret_cast<const void*>(&UhcTechSubtypeLookupHook), 5) ||
        !uhc::WriteCall(kAttributeCall, reinterpret_cast<const void*>(&UhcTechSubtypeAttrHook)) ||
        !uhc::WriteJmp(kApplySwitch, reinterpret_cast<const void*>(&UhcTechSubtypeApplyHook), 9) ||
        !uhc::WriteJmp(kUnapplySwitch, reinterpret_cast<const void*>(&UhcTechSubtypeUnapplyHook), 9)) {
      return false;
    }
    g_installed = true;
  }
  return true;
}

bool Add(const Subtype& subtype) {
  if (g_count == kMaxSubtypes) {
    uhc::Log(L"tech subtypes: no room for %hs", subtype.name);
    return false;
  }
  if (!Install()) {
    return false;
  }
  g_subtypes[g_count++] = subtype;
  return true;
}


}  // namespace

// The head of the lookup. Its head, not the call in setData: another DLL may replace that call, and what
// it does not know still ends here.
extern "C" std::int32_t __stdcall UhcTechSubtypeLookup(void* db, const char* name, std::uint32_t returnAddress) {
  const std::int32_t stock = kNameLookup(db, name);
  if (stock != -1 || name == nullptr || returnAddress == kOtherCaller) {
    return stock;
  }
  for (std::size_t i = 0; i < g_count; ++i) {
    if (_stricmp(name, g_subtypes[i].name) != 0) {
      continue;
    }
    if (g_subtypes[i].actionHandler != nullptr && !ReadAction(db, name, g_uhcSubtypeEffect, g_uhcSubtypeNode)) {
      return -1;
    }
    if (g_subtypes[i].typeHandler != nullptr && !ReadType(db, name, g_uhcSubtypeEffect, g_uhcSubtypeNode)) {
      return -1;
    }
    return kFirstId + static_cast<std::int32_t>(i);
  }
  if (g_unknown != nullptr) {
    g_unknown(name);
  }
  return -1;
}

// From the two switch hooks, for a subtype at or above kFirstId. False: stock's failure exit.
extern "C" bool __stdcall UhcTechSubtypeRun(std::int32_t id, char* proto, const char* base, float amount,
                                            std::uint32_t relativity, std::uint32_t unapply, std::int32_t actionId,
                                            std::uint32_t allActions) {
  const std::size_t index = static_cast<std::size_t>(id - kFirstId);
  if (index >= g_count) {
    return false;
  }
  const Subtype& subtype = g_subtypes[index];
  if (subtype.typeHandler != nullptr) {
    subtype.typeHandler(proto, base, actionId, unapply != 0);
    return true;
  }
  if (subtype.actionHandler != nullptr) {
    return RunOnActions(subtype, proto, base, amount, static_cast<std::uint8_t>(relativity), unapply != 0, actionId,
                        allActions != 0);
  }
  subtype.handler(proto, base, amount, static_cast<std::uint8_t>(relativity), unapply != 0);
  return true;
}

namespace uhc {

bool AddTechSubtype(const char* name, TechSubtypeHandler handler) { return Add({name, handler, nullptr, nullptr}); }

bool AddTechActionSubtype(const char* name, TechActionSubtypeHandler handler) {
  return Add({name, nullptr, handler, nullptr});
}

bool AddTechTypeSubtype(const char* name, TechTypeSubtypeHandler handler) {
  return Add({name, nullptr, nullptr, handler});
}

bool OnUnknownTechSubtype(UnknownTechSubtype callback) {
  if (!Install()) {
    return false;
  }
  g_unknown = callback;
  return true;
}

}  // namespace uhc
```

### src/hooks/techsubtype_hooks.asm

```asm
; The shared hooks for new Data effect subtypes (src/techsubtypes.h). Entered through a jmp written
; over the stock code; they leave through an explicit jmp to a verified continuation.
.686
.model flat, c
option casemap :none

include common.inc

UhcTechSubtypeLookup PROTO STDCALL db_:DWORD, subtypeName:DWORD, returnAddress:DWORD
EXTERN g_uhcSubtypeEffect:DWORD
EXTERN g_uhcSubtypeNode:DWORD
UhcTechSubtypeRun PROTO STDCALL subtype:DWORD, target:DWORD, base:DWORD, amount:DWORD, relativity:DWORD, unapply:DWORD, actionId:DWORD, allActions:DWORD

.data
xmlAttribute      dd 00598F37h    ; the XML attribute read
lookupNext        dd 0061FF62h    ; test edi,edi, in the name lookup
applySwitchNext   dd 008F9796h    ; movzx ecx, the subtype's jump index
applyDone         dd 008FAE9Eh    ; pop edi / esi / ebp, mov al,1, pop ebx / ecx, ret 20h
applyFail         dd 008FAEF4h    ; the same with xor al,al
unapplySwitchNext dd 008FB05Ah    ; movzx eax, the subtype's jump index
unapplyDone       dd 008FC191h    ; pop edi / esi / ebp, mov al,1, pop ebx, ret 20h
unapplyFail       dd 008FC19Ah    ; the same with xor al,al

.code

; Replaces the call at 0x6206B2 in BDataEffect::setData, which reads the attribute subtype. ESI = the
; effect, EBP = its XML node: kept here because another DLL may do the rest of the lookup its own way.
UhcTechSubtypeAttrHook PROC
    mov g_uhcSubtypeEffect, esi
    mov g_uhcSubtypeNode, ebp
    jmp xmlAttribute
UhcTechSubtypeAttrHook ENDP

; The head of the name lookup 0x61FF5D, thiscall(db, name), ret 4.
UhcTechSubtypeLookupHook PROC
    TRACE_ONCE "tech subtype lookup (0x61FF5D)"
    mov eax, dword ptr [esp]
    mov edx, dword ptr [esp+4]
    invoke UhcTechSubtypeLookup, ecx, edx, eax
    ret 4
UhcTechSubtypeLookupHook ENDP

; The stock lookup: re-executes 0x61FF5D..0x61FF61 (push edi / mov edi,[esp+8]).
UhcTechSubtypeLookupStock PROC
    push edi
    mov edi, dword ptr [esp+8]
    jmp lookupNext
UhcTechSubtypeLookupStock ENDP

; Replaces 0x8F978D..0x8F9795 (cmp eax,63h / ja 0x8FAEF4) in applyData. EAX = subtype, EDI = target
; proto, ESI = its base proto, [esp+20h] the action's ID, [esp+28h] amount, [esp+2Ch] relativity,
; [esp+34h] the allactions flag, the function's arguments 3, 5, 6 and 8 (0x7F86C0).
UhcTechSubtypeApplyHook PROC
    TRACE_ONCE "tech subtype apply (0x8F978D)"
    cmp eax, 100h
    jae custom
    cmp eax, 63h
    ja fail
    jmp applySwitchNext
custom:
    movzx ecx, byte ptr [esp+34h]
    push ecx                        ; allActions
    push dword ptr [esp+24h]        ; actionId, 20h + the push
    push 0                          ; unapply
    movzx ecx, byte ptr [esp+38h]   ; relativity, 2Ch + 3 pushes
    push ecx
    push dword ptr [esp+38h]        ; amount, 28h + 4 pushes
    push esi
    push edi
    push eax
    call UhcTechSubtypeRun
    test al, al
    je fail
    jmp applyDone
fail:
    jmp applyFail
UhcTechSubtypeApplyHook ENDP

; Replaces 0x8FB051..0x8FB059 (cmp eax,5Eh / ja 0x8FC19A) in unapplyData. EAX = subtype, ESI = target
; proto, EDI = its base proto, [esp+1Ch] the action's ID, [esp+24h] amount, [esp+28h] relativity,
; [esp+30h] the allactions flag: the same arguments, with one register less saved.
UhcTechSubtypeUnapplyHook PROC
    TRACE_ONCE "tech subtype unapply (0x8FB051)"
    cmp eax, 100h
    jae custom
    cmp eax, 5Eh
    ja fail
    jmp unapplySwitchNext
custom:
    movzx ecx, byte ptr [esp+30h]
    push ecx                        ; allActions
    push dword ptr [esp+20h]        ; actionId, 1Ch + the push
    push 1                          ; unapply
    movzx ecx, byte ptr [esp+34h]   ; relativity, 28h + 3 pushes
    push ecx
    push dword ptr [esp+34h]        ; amount, 24h + 4 pushes
    push edi
    push esi
    push eax
    call UhcTechSubtypeRun
    test al, al
    je fail
    jmp unapplyDone
fail:
    jmp unapplyFail
UhcTechSubtypeUnapplyHook ENDP

END
```

## Modify types

### src/modifytypes.h

```cpp
// One hook on the tail of the ModifyType name parser FUN_005F5645 (0x5FE6D7), shared by every key
// that adds a name to <ModifyType>: a single jmp, whichever of those keys are on.
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

namespace uhc {

// Makes `name` (static text; case ignored, like every stock name) read as `stat`. The stock stats
// are 0..8 and 0Bh..0Eh; the keys use 40h and up. Writes the hook the first time, after checking
// the stock bytes.
bool AddModifyType(const wchar_t* name, std::int32_t stat);

// Called for a name nothing knows, while the data loads, for dataLint. `proto` is null when the name was not read in a
// proto's action. Writes the hook too.
using UnknownModifyType = void (*)(const wchar_t* name, const char* proto);
bool OnUnknownModifyType(UnknownModifyType callback);

}  // namespace uhc
```

### src/modifytypes.cpp

```cpp
#include "modifytypes.h"

#include <cstddef>

#include "generated/sites.h"
#include "log.h"
#include "patch.h"

extern "C" void UhcModifyTypeHook();  // hooks/modifytype_hooks.asm

namespace {

constexpr std::size_t kMaxTypes = 16;
constexpr std::uint32_t kNameTailHook = 0x005FE6D7;  // neg eax / sbb eax,eax / and al,0F1h (6 B)
constexpr std::int32_t kTailAdds = 0x0E;             // the tail adds 0Eh to what the hook leaves in EAX
constexpr std::uint32_t kFromActionParser = 0x005FC8B7;  // the action parser's call of the name parser

using CompareFn = std::int32_t(__thiscall*)(const void* name, const wchar_t* text, std::int32_t flag, std::int32_t n);
const auto kCompare = reinterpret_cast<CompareFn>(0x00443C10);

struct ModifyType {
  const wchar_t* name;
  std::int32_t stat;
};

ModifyType g_types[kMaxTypes] = {};
std::size_t g_count = 0;
bool g_installed = false;
uhc::UnknownModifyType g_unknown = nullptr;

bool Install() {
  if (g_installed) {
    return true;
  }
  if (!uhc::VerifyAll("modify types", {&uhc::sites::kModifyTypeNameTail, &uhc::sites::kModifyTypeNameCompare}) ||
      !uhc::WriteJmp(kNameTailHook, reinterpret_cast<const void*>(&UhcModifyTypeHook), 6)) {
    return false;
  }
  g_installed = true;
  return true;
}

}  // namespace

// From the hook, for a name that is none of the stock ones. The tail returns EAX + 0Eh. For the action parser's call,
// `proto` is its argument; for the other caller that slot means nothing.
extern "C" std::int32_t __stdcall UhcModifyTypeByName(const void* name, std::uint32_t returnAddress, const char* proto) {
  for (std::size_t i = 0; i < g_count; ++i) {
    if (kCompare(name, g_types[i].name, 0, -1) == 0) {
      return g_types[i].stat - kTailAdds;
    }
  }
  if (g_unknown != nullptr) {
    g_unknown(*static_cast<const wchar_t* const*>(name), returnAddress == kFromActionParser ? proto : nullptr);
  }
  return -1 - kTailAdds;
}

namespace uhc {

bool AddModifyType(const wchar_t* name, std::int32_t stat) {
  if (g_count == kMaxTypes) {
    Log(L"modify types: no room for %ls", name);
    return false;
  }
  if (!Install()) {
    return false;
  }
  g_types[g_count++] = {name, stat};
  return true;
}

bool OnUnknownModifyType(UnknownModifyType callback) {
  if (!Install()) {
    return false;
  }
  g_unknown = callback;
  return true;
}

}  // namespace uhc
```

### src/hooks/modifytype_hooks.asm

```asm
; The shared hook for new ModifyType names (src/modifytypes.h). Entered through a jmp written over
; the stock code; it leaves through an explicit jmp to a verified continuation.
.686
.model flat, c
option casemap :none

include common.inc

UhcModifyTypeByName PROTO STDCALL :DWORD, :DWORD, :DWORD

.data
nameTailNext    dd 005FE6DDh    ; pop esi / add eax,0Eh / ret: FUN_005F5645 returns EAX + 0Eh

.code

; Replaces 0x5FE6D7..0x5FE6DC (neg eax / sbb eax,eax / and al,0F1h) in FUN_005F5645, the
; ModifyType name -> stat parser, after its last compare. EAX = that compare's result for
; "AutoGatherRate" (0: the same name), ESI = the name (BString*), [esp] = the ESI the function
; saved. Stock turns EAX into 0 (a match) or -0Fh and the tail adds 0Eh: stat 0Eh, or -1 for an
; unknown name. The hook keeps a match; for any other name the helper returns the stat - 0Eh, or
; -0Fh. ECX, EDX and the flags are dead at the tail, which sets the flags, and its two callers read
; only EAX. [esp+4] = the caller; for the action parser, its proto argument is at [esp+0D0h] (0C4h in its loop, with
; the return address and the argument of this call on top, and the saved ESI).
UhcModifyTypeHook PROC
    TRACE_ONCE "ModifyType names (0x5FE6D7)"
    test eax, eax
    je done                         ; "AutoGatherRate": EAX = 0, the tail returns 0Eh
    push dword ptr [esp+0D0h]
    push dword ptr [esp+8]
    push esi
    call UhcModifyTypeByName
done:
    jmp nameTailNext
UhcModifyTypeHook ENDP

END
```

## Floating text

### src/floatingtext.h

```cpp
// The stock floating text over a unit (the dodge's, string 63950, 0x6A24FC..0x6A2608), for keys
// that show something to the local player: noTrainNearEnemyTC's warning, damageOverTime's ticks.
// Interface only: it reads the local player and writes only the text manager.
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

namespace uhc {

// Checks the stock code the helper repeats (logged under feature's name).
bool VerifyFloatingText(const char* feature);

// The local player's ID, [[game]+3C0h]; -1 without a game.
std::int32_t LocalPlayer();

// True when the text manager [world+8A8h] exists and unit is visible to the local player
// (0x4818F4), the dodge's conditions for its text.
bool CanShowUnitText(const char* unit);

// Shows text (or, with stringId >= 0, that string table entry) over unit, in colour (ARGB), when
// CanShowUnitText. The text does not rise. Returns the manager's index, or -1 when nothing was
// shown (the engine also refuses an empty text).
std::int32_t ShowUnitText(const char* unit, const wchar_t* text, std::int32_t stringId, std::uint32_t colour);

}  // namespace uhc
```

### src/floatingtext.cpp

```cpp
#include "floatingtext.h"

#include "generated/sites.h"
#include "patch.h"

namespace uhc {
namespace {

// Engine layout (docs/engine-symbols.md, "Training near an enemy Town Center").
constexpr std::uint32_t kGameGlobal = 0x00C66234;  // game; +13Ch world, +3C0h local player

// The engine's string: one pointer to a wide, reference-counted buffer.
struct BString {
  wchar_t* text;
};

using VisibleToFn = bool(__thiscall*)(const char* unit, std::int32_t playerId);
using ConfigIntFn = bool(__thiscall*)(void* config, std::int32_t id, std::int32_t* out);
using FloatingTextFn = std::int32_t(__thiscall*)(char* manager, const BString* text, std::int32_t time1Ms,
                                                 std::int32_t time2Ms, std::int32_t unitId, const float* motion,
                                                 std::uint32_t colour, float scale, std::int32_t zero,
                                                 std::int32_t minusOne);
using BStringFromWideFn = BString*(__thiscall*)(BString* self, const wchar_t* text);
using BStringFromIdFn = BString*(__thiscall*)(BString* self, std::int32_t stringId);
using BStringFreeFn = void(__thiscall*)(BString* self);

const auto kVisibleTo = reinterpret_cast<VisibleToFn>(0x004818F4);
const auto kConfigInt = reinterpret_cast<ConfigIntFn>(0x0046A144);
const auto kFloatingText = reinterpret_cast<FloatingTextFn>(0x008023AE);
const auto kBStringFromWide = reinterpret_cast<BStringFromWideFn>(0x00401A12);
const auto kBStringFromId = reinterpret_cast<BStringFromIdFn>(0x0058EA52);
const auto kBStringFree = reinterpret_cast<BStringFreeFn>(0x004010CA);
void* const kConfig = reinterpret_cast<void*>(0x00C66500);
// The config variables the dodge's floating text reads for its two times in ms (pointers to their
// IDs, 0x6A257B..0x6A25A6; 2000 and 1000 in game, as the stock caller 0x9C039E passes).
constexpr std::uint32_t kTextTime1 = 0x00CEFFF8;
constexpr std::uint32_t kTextTime2 = 0x00CF0000;

template <typename T>
T Field(const char* base, std::uint32_t offset) {
  return *reinterpret_cast<const T*>(base + offset);
}

const char* Game() { return *reinterpret_cast<const char* const*>(kGameGlobal); }

char* TextManager() {
  const char* world = Game() == nullptr ? nullptr : Field<const char*>(Game(), 0x13C);
  return world == nullptr ? nullptr : Field<char*>(world, 0x8A8);
}

}  // namespace

bool VerifyFloatingText(const char* feature) {
  return VerifyAll(feature, {&sites::kDodgeFloatingText, &sites::kFloatingTextAdd, &sites::kUnitVisibleTo,
                             &sites::kConfigInt, &sites::kBStringFromWide, &sites::kBStringFromId,
                             &sites::kBStringFree});
}

std::int32_t LocalPlayer() { return Game() == nullptr ? -1 : Field<std::int32_t>(Game(), 0x3C0); }

bool CanShowUnitText(const char* unit) {
  return unit != nullptr && TextManager() != nullptr && kVisibleTo(unit, LocalPlayer());
}

std::int32_t ShowUnitText(const char* unit, const wchar_t* text, std::int32_t stringId, std::uint32_t colour) {
  if (!CanShowUnitText(unit)) {
    return -1;
  }
  // Three floats. The stock callers pass {0, 0.1, 0}: the second makes the text rise, and in the
  // test bed it left the screen in about 0.3 s. At 0 it stays over the unit for its whole life.
  const float motion[3] = {0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f};
  std::int32_t time1 = 1000;
  std::int32_t time2 = 2000;
  kConfigInt(kConfig, **reinterpret_cast<const std::int32_t* const*>(kTextTime1), &time1);
  kConfigInt(kConfig, **reinterpret_cast<const std::int32_t* const*>(kTextTime2), &time2);
  BString string;
  if (stringId >= 0) {
    kBStringFromId(&string, stringId);
  } else {
    kBStringFromWide(&string, text);
  }
  const std::int32_t shown =
      kFloatingText(TextManager(), &string, time1, time2, Field<std::int32_t>(unit, 0x10), motion, colour, 1.0f, 0, -1);
  kBStringFree(&string);
  return shown;
}

}  // namespace uhc
```

## Death observers

### src/deathobservers.h

```cpp
// Every death of a unit, shared by the keys that act on it (detonationDeath, annexFacing). The kill queue's
// drain 0x493D46 calls the kill handler 0x4B4F70 for each unit asked to die (TakeDamage, a lifespan, a delete, a
// trigger's kill and the rest ask 0x4B4DC2), from a single call, 0x493D8F. One call is written there, whichever
// keys are on, and it runs every observer before the stock handler.
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

namespace uhc {

// Called with the unit and the handler's three flags, before the stock handler, while the unit is whole. The
// handler returns at once for a unit whose state bit 11 is set, so an observer that needs a real death checks it.
// A kill asked from an observer joins the queue, and the drain reads its count again: the unit dies in the same
// drain, after this one, with no recursion.
using DeathObserver = void (*)(char* unit, std::uint32_t a, std::uint32_t b, std::uint32_t c);

// Writes the call the first time (after checking the site's stock bytes) and adds the observer, in the order the
// keys add them (their kFeatures order, the same on every machine).
bool AddDeathObserver(DeathObserver observer);

// The trigger effect Destroy (the syscall trUnitDestroy, 0x98282F) removes each selected unit at once through
// the world's unit removal 0x4726B2(id, 0, 1), from its single call 0x9828E7, and never reaches the kill queue.
// An observer added here is called there, with the flags 0 0 0, before the removal and only for a unit that is
// alive and not dead (state bit 11 clear). It is a separate list: a key that should act on Destroy adds its
// observer to both. The removal's 62 other callers (a corpse's end, a transform's old unit, a game's end, the
// editor) are not observed.
bool AddDestroyObserver(DeathObserver observer);

}  // namespace uhc
```

### src/deathobservers.cpp

```cpp
#include "deathobservers.h"

#include <cstddef>

#include "generated/sites.h"
#include "log.h"
#include "patch.h"

namespace {

constexpr std::size_t kMaxObservers = 8;

using KillFn = void(__thiscall*)(char* unit, std::uint32_t a, std::uint32_t b, std::uint32_t c);
const auto kKill = reinterpret_cast<KillFn>(0x004B4F70);

uhc::DeathObserver g_observers[kMaxObservers] = {};
std::size_t g_count = 0;
bool g_installed = false;

using RemoveFn = void(__thiscall*)(char* world, std::int32_t id, std::int32_t a, std::int32_t b);
const auto kRemove = reinterpret_cast<RemoveFn>(0x004726B2);
using UnitTestFn = bool(__thiscall*)(const char* unit);

uhc::DeathObserver g_destroyObservers[kMaxObservers] = {};
std::size_t g_destroyCount = 0;
bool g_destroyInstalled = false;

char* UnitById(const char* world, std::int32_t id) {
  if (world == nullptr || id < 0 || (id & 0x3FFFF) >= *reinterpret_cast<const std::int32_t*>(world + 0x10)) {
    return nullptr;
  }
  char* unit = (*reinterpret_cast<char* const* const*>(world + 0x0C))[id & 0x3FFFF];
  return unit != nullptr && *reinterpret_cast<const std::int32_t*>(unit + 0x10) == id ? unit : nullptr;
}

bool IsDead(const char* unit) {  // state bit 11
  const auto* bits = *reinterpret_cast<const std::uint8_t* const*>(unit + 4);
  return bits != nullptr && *reinterpret_cast<const std::uint32_t*>(unit + 8) > 11 && (bits[1] & 0x08) != 0;
}

// Replaces trUnitDestroy's call of the unit removal: thiscall(world, id, a, b), ret 0Ch.
void __fastcall Destroy(char* world, void*, std::int32_t id, std::int32_t a, std::int32_t b) {
  static bool traced = false;
  if (!traced) {
    traced = true;
    uhc::Log(L"hook reached: unit destroys (0x9828E7)");
  }
  char* unit = UnitById(world, id);
  if (unit != nullptr && !IsDead(unit) && (*reinterpret_cast<UnitTestFn const* const*>(unit))[0xA4 / 4](unit)) {
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < g_destroyCount; ++i) {
      g_destroyObservers[i](unit, 0, 0, 0);
    }
  }
  kRemove(world, id, a, b);
}

// Replaces the drain's call of the kill handler: thiscall(unit, a, b, c), ret 0Ch, like the handler.
void __fastcall Kill(char* unit, void*, std::uint32_t a, std::uint32_t b, std::uint32_t c) {
  static bool traced = false;
  if (!traced) {
    traced = true;
    uhc::Log(L"hook reached: unit deaths (0x493D8F)");
  }
  for (std::size_t i = 0; i < g_count; ++i) {
    g_observers[i](unit, a, b, c);
  }
  kKill(unit, a, b, c);
}

}  // namespace

namespace uhc {

bool AddDeathObserver(DeathObserver observer) {
  if (g_count == kMaxObservers) {
    Log(L"unit deaths: more than %u observers", static_cast<unsigned>(kMaxObservers));
    return false;
  }
  if (!g_installed) {
    if (!VerifyAll("unit deaths", {&sites::kDetonationKillCall}) ||
        !WriteCall(sites::kDetonationKillCall.va, reinterpret_cast<const void*>(&Kill))) {
      return false;
    }
    g_installed = true;
  }
  g_observers[g_count++] = observer;
  return true;
}

bool AddDestroyObserver(DeathObserver observer) {
  if (g_destroyCount == kMaxObservers) {
    Log(L"unit destroys: more than %u observers", static_cast<unsigned>(kMaxObservers));
    return false;
  }
  if (!g_destroyInstalled) {
    if (!VerifyAll("unit destroys", {&sites::kDestroyRemoveCall, &sites::kUnitRemoveHead}) ||
        !WriteCall(sites::kDestroyRemoveCall.va, reinterpret_cast<const void*>(&Destroy))) {
      return false;
    }
    g_destroyInstalled = true;
  }
  g_destroyObservers[g_destroyCount++] = observer;
  return true;
}

}  // namespace uhc
```

## Modifier walk

### src/modifiers.h

```cpp
// Stat modifiers on units, read-only (docs/engine-symbols.md, "Snare and group movement"). A
// modifier is a unit action of type 2Ch in the unit's action lists, created by 0x4DD22D ->
// 0x4B3D9E (snares, auras, powers) or by the tactics-driven class 0xB97C78, which carries the same
// type: stat [+64h], value [+68h], operation [+78h] (0 set, 1 add, 2 multiply), flags
// {[+30h] bytes, [+34h] count}. Shared by snareOnlyHitUnit and buildingWorkRateAuras.
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

namespace uhc::modifiers {

constexpr std::uint8_t kModifierAction = 0x2C;  // unit action type of a stat modifier (0x4B3D9E)
constexpr int kMaxDepth = 16;                   // nested actions; a deeper tree is not walked

enum class Walk { kDone, kStopped, kTooDeep };

namespace detail {

template <typename T>
T Field(const char* base, std::uint32_t offset) {
  return *reinterpret_cast<const T*>(base + offset);
}

}  // namespace detail

// Calls visit(action) for each modifier action in an action list {count +0, data +8} and, depth
// first, in each action's children ([action+40h]): the order of the stock collector
// 0x49F1A0 / 0x49F34B. visit returns false to stop the walk.
template <typename Visit>
Walk WalkModifiers(const char* list, Visit& visit, int depth) {
  if (depth > kMaxDepth) {
    return Walk::kTooDeep;
  }
  const std::int32_t count = detail::Field<std::int32_t>(list, 0);
  const char* const* actions = detail::Field<const char* const*>(list, 8);
  for (std::int32_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
    const char* action = actions[i];
    if (action == nullptr) {
      continue;
    }
    if (detail::Field<std::uint8_t>(action, 0x60) == kModifierAction && !visit(action)) {
      return Walk::kStopped;
    }
    const Walk inner = WalkModifiers(action + 0x40, visit, depth + 1);
    if (inner != Walk::kDone) {
      return inner;
    }
  }
  return Walk::kDone;
}

// The unit's modifier actions: the list at [actionManager+14h], as FUN_0049F1BA collects them.
inline const char* ModifierList(const char* unit) {
  const char* manager = detail::Field<const char*>(unit, 0x58);
  return manager == nullptr ? nullptr : manager + 0x14;
}

}  // namespace uhc::modifiers
```

## Unit panel row (the minimized interface)

### src/unitpanel.h

```cpp
// One hook after the unit panel's own AutoGather rate (0x699456), shared by every key that writes in
// that row of the minimized interface (showGatherRate, overhealHitpointBars stat): with
// optionminimizedui the exe skips the stat panel fill, and these two gadgets are the row it has left.
#pragma once

namespace uhc {

// Called while one unit is selected, for that unit. Writes the row and returns true, or returns
// false to leave it to the next key. The last key added is asked first.
using UnitPanelRow = bool(__stdcall*)(const char* panel, const char* unit);

// The row's text, in the style of the stock AutoGather row; false if this interface has no such gadgets.
bool SetUnitPanelRow(const char* panel, const wchar_t* label, const wchar_t* value);

// Writes the hook the first time (after checking the site's stock bytes) and adds row.
bool AddUnitPanelRow(UnitPanelRow row);

// One key may also write the row for a selection of several units: `row` gets the panel and the
// selection group (IDs [group+10h], count [group+14h]). The single-unit rows do not run for a group.
using UnitPanelGroupRow = bool(__stdcall*)(const char* panel, const char* selection);
void SetUnitPanelGroupRow(UnitPanelGroupRow row);

}  // namespace uhc
```

### src/unitpanel.cpp

```cpp
#include "unitpanel.h"

#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>

#include "generated/sites.h"
#include "log.h"
#include "patch.h"

namespace {

constexpr std::size_t kMaxRows = 8;

// [panel+33Ch] unitResourceRateLabel, [panel+340h] unitResourceRateValue, and the styles of the
// stock row (vtable +84h), then the text (+80h) and the refresh (+54h).
constexpr std::uint32_t kPanelRateLabel = 0x33C;
constexpr std::uint32_t kPanelRateValue = 0x340;
constexpr std::uint32_t kLabelStyle = 0x00D6F5D0;
constexpr std::uint32_t kValueStyle = 0x00C6657C;

struct BString {
  wchar_t* text;
};

using GadgetRefFn = std::int32_t*(__thiscall*)(std::int32_t* ref, std::int32_t handle);  // ref: handle, gadget
using GadgetReleaseFn = std::int32_t(__cdecl*)(std::int32_t handle);
using GadgetStyleFn = void(__thiscall*)(void* gadget, std::uint32_t style);
using GadgetTextFn = void(__thiscall*)(void* gadget, const BString* text);
using GadgetUpdateFn = void(__thiscall*)(void* gadget);
using BStringFromWideFn = BString*(__thiscall*)(BString* self, const wchar_t* text);
using BStringFreeFn = void(__thiscall*)(BString* self);
using CurrentSelectionFn = const char*(__cdecl*)();  // reads globals only; the stock callers' ECX is unused
const auto kGadgetRef = reinterpret_cast<GadgetRefFn>(0x004C519C);
const auto kGadgetRelease = reinterpret_cast<GadgetReleaseFn>(0x0040431B);
const auto kBStringFromWide = reinterpret_cast<BStringFromWideFn>(0x00401A12);
const auto kBStringFree = reinterpret_cast<BStringFreeFn>(0x004010CA);
const auto kCurrentSelection = reinterpret_cast<CurrentSelectionFn>(0x00453B28);

template <typename T>
T Field(const char* base, std::uint32_t offset) {
  return *reinterpret_cast<const T*>(base + offset);
}

template <typename Fn>
Fn VirtualAt(void* object, std::uint32_t offset) {
  return *reinterpret_cast<Fn*>(*reinterpret_cast<char**>(object) + offset);
}

// The gadget behind a handle; the reference is released at once, as stock does.
void* PanelGadget(const char* panel, std::uint32_t field) {
  std::int32_t ref[2] = {0, 0};
  std::int32_t* result = kGadgetRef(ref, Field<std::int32_t>(panel, field));
  void* gadget = reinterpret_cast<void*>(static_cast<std::intptr_t>(result[1]));
  if (gadget != nullptr) {
    kGadgetRelease(result[0]);
  }
  return gadget;
}

void SetGadget(void* gadget, std::uint32_t style, const wchar_t* text) {
  BString value{nullptr};
  kBStringFromWide(&value, text);
  VirtualAt<GadgetStyleFn>(gadget, 0x84)(gadget, style);
  VirtualAt<GadgetTextFn>(gadget, 0x80)(gadget, &value);
  VirtualAt<GadgetUpdateFn>(gadget, 0x54)(gadget);
  kBStringFree(&value);
}

uhc::UnitPanelRow g_rows[kMaxRows] = {};
std::size_t g_rowCount = 0;
uhc::UnitPanelGroupRow g_groupRow = nullptr;
bool g_installed = false;

}  // namespace

extern "C" {
void UhcUnitPanelHook();  // hooks/unitpanelrow_hooks.asm

// From the hook at 0x699456. One unit selected, as the stat panel's rows ask: with a group the panel
// shows the first unit, and its numbers would read as the group's.
void __stdcall UhcUnitPanelRows(const char* panel, const char* unit) {
  if (panel == nullptr || unit == nullptr) {
    return;
  }
  const char* selection = kCurrentSelection();
  if (selection == nullptr || Field<std::uint8_t>(selection, 0x21) != 0) {
    return;
  }
  if (Field<std::int32_t>(selection, 0x14) != 1) {
    if (g_groupRow != nullptr) {
      g_groupRow(panel, selection);
    }
    return;
  }
  for (std::size_t i = g_rowCount; i > 0; --i) {
    if (g_rows[i - 1](panel, unit)) {
      return;
    }
  }
}
}

namespace uhc {

bool SetUnitPanelRow(const char* panel, const wchar_t* label, const wchar_t* value) {
  void* labelGadget = PanelGadget(panel, kPanelRateLabel);
```
