```cpp
  void* valueGadget = PanelGadget(panel, kPanelRateValue);
  if (labelGadget == nullptr || valueGadget == nullptr) {
    return false;
  }
  SetGadget(labelGadget, kLabelStyle, label);
  SetGadget(valueGadget, kValueStyle, value);
  return true;
}

void SetUnitPanelGroupRow(UnitPanelGroupRow row) { g_groupRow = row; }

bool AddUnitPanelRow(UnitPanelRow row) {
  if (g_rowCount == kMaxRows) {
    Log(L"unit panel: more than %u rows", static_cast<unsigned>(kMaxRows));
    return false;
  }
  if (!g_installed) {
    if (!VerifyAll("unit panel", {&sites::kUnitPanelRateRow, &sites::kUnitPanelAfterRate, &sites::kCurrentSelectionGroup,
                                  &sites::kGadgetRef, &sites::kGadgetRelease, &sites::kBStringFromWide,
                                  &sites::kBStringFree}) ||
        !WriteJmp(sites::kUnitPanelAfterRate.va, reinterpret_cast<const void*>(&UhcUnitPanelHook), 6)) {
      return false;
    }
    g_installed = true;
  }
  g_rows[g_rowCount++] = row;
  return true;
}

}  // namespace uhc
```

### src/hooks/unitpanelrow_hooks.asm

```asm
; The unit panel's shared row (unitpanel.cpp). Entered through a jmp written over the stock code.
.686
.model flat, c
option casemap :none

include common.inc

UhcUnitPanelRows PROTO STDCALL panel:DWORD, unit:DWORD

.data
unitPanelNext   dd 0069945Ch    ; call 4826B5h: the unit panel goes on, after the stolen load

.code

; Replaces 0x699456..0x69945B (mov edi,[esp+54h] / mov ecx,edi) in the unit panel's cold part, the
; join point after its own AutoGather rate row ([esp+50h] = the panel, [esp+54h] = the unit). The
; stat panel fill does not run with the minimized interface; this is where that interface's row
; is written. Nothing else changes.
UhcUnitPanelHook PROC
    TRACE_ONCE "unit panel row (0x699456)"
    pushad
    push dword ptr [esp+20h+54h]        ; the unit
    push dword ptr [esp+24h+50h]        ; the panel
    call UhcUnitPanelRows
    popad
    mov edi, dword ptr [esp+54h]        ; stolen 0x699456
    mov ecx, edi                        ; stolen 0x69945A
    jmp unitPanelNext
UhcUnitPanelHook ENDP

END
```

## Name lists

### src/namelists.h

```cpp
// Name lists from uhc.cfg resolved against the game data, lazily. civs.xml is read again with the
// rest of the data when a skirmish starts, so a list is resolved on first use and again whenever
// the database or its civ count changes. Called on the game's main thread only.
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

namespace uhc {

class CivIdList {
 public:
  // `feature` names the key in the log lines.
  void Set(const char* feature, std::vector<std::string> names);
  bool empty() const { return names_.empty(); }
  const std::vector<std::string>& names() const { return names_; }

  // The position in the cfg list of the civ with this ID, or -1 (also before civs.xml is read).
  int IndexOf(std::int32_t civ);

 private:
  void Resolve(char* database, std::int32_t count);

  const char* feature_ = "";
  std::vector<std::string> names_;
  std::vector<int> index_;  // civ ID -> position in names_, or -1
  const char* database_ = nullptr;
  std::int32_t count_ = -1;
};

class ProtoIdList {
 public:
  void Set(const char* feature, std::vector<std::string> names);
  bool empty() const { return names_.empty(); }
  // The proto IDs of the names that exist in the current data (empty before it is read).
  const std::vector<std::int32_t>& Ids();
  // The position in the cfg list of the proto with this ID, or -1.
  int IndexOf(std::int32_t proto);

 private:
  bool Refresh();

  const char* feature_ = "";
  std::vector<std::string> names_;
  std::vector<std::int32_t> ids_;       // resolved IDs, in cfg order, unknown names left out
  std::vector<std::int32_t> idOfName_;  // per cfg name, -1 if unknown
  const char* database_ = nullptr;
  std::int32_t count_ = -1;
};

// The first civ ID of a player object ([player+20h] list, [player+24h] count), or -1.
std::int32_t FirstCivOfPlayer(const char* player);

}  // namespace uhc
```

### src/namelists.cpp

```cpp
#include "namelists.h"

#include "log.h"

namespace uhc {
namespace {

constexpr std::uint32_t kGameGlobal = 0x00C66234;  // game; +140h database

// int thiscall(database, const char* name): the civ's ID (its index in [database+24h]), or -1.
using CivIdByNameFn = std::int32_t(__thiscall*)(char* database, const char* name);
const auto kCivIdByName = reinterpret_cast<CivIdByNameFn>(0x0058E71F);

// int thiscall(database, const char* name): the proto's ID, or -1.
using ProtoIdByNameFn = std::int32_t(__thiscall*)(char* database, const char* name);
const auto kProtoIdByName = reinterpret_cast<ProtoIdByNameFn>(0x005A2346);

template <typename T>
T Field(const char* base, std::uint32_t offset) {
  return *reinterpret_cast<const T*>(base + offset);
}

}  // namespace

void CivIdList::Set(const char* feature, std::vector<std::string> names) {
  feature_ = feature;
  names_ = std::move(names);
  database_ = nullptr;
  count_ = -1;
}

void CivIdList::Resolve(char* database, std::int32_t count) {
  database_ = database;
  count_ = count;
  index_.assign(static_cast<std::size_t>(count), -1);
  for (std::size_t i = 0; i < names_.size(); ++i) {
    const std::int32_t id = kCivIdByName(database, names_[i].c_str());
    if (id < 0 || id >= count) {
      Log(L"%hs: %hs is not a civ in civs.xml; ignored", feature_, names_[i].c_str());
      continue;
    }
    if (index_[static_cast<std::size_t>(id)] < 0) {
      index_[static_cast<std::size_t>(id)] = static_cast<int>(i);
    }
    Log(L"%hs: %hs -> civ %d", feature_, names_[i].c_str(), id);
  }
}

int CivIdList::IndexOf(std::int32_t civ) {
  if (civ < 0 || names_.empty()) {
    return -1;
  }
  char* game = *reinterpret_cast<char**>(kGameGlobal);
  char* database = game == nullptr ? nullptr : Field<char*>(game, 0x140);
  if (database == nullptr) {
    return -1;
  }
  const std::int32_t count = Field<std::int32_t>(database, 0x28);
  if (count <= 0) {
    return -1;  // civs.xml not read yet
  }
  if (database != database_ || count != count_) {
    Resolve(database, count);
  }
  return civ < count ? index_[static_cast<std::size_t>(civ)] : -1;
}

void ProtoIdList::Set(const char* feature, std::vector<std::string> names) {
  feature_ = feature;
  names_ = std::move(names);
  database_ = nullptr;
  count_ = -1;
}

// Protos: [database+54h] array, [database+58h] count.
bool ProtoIdList::Refresh() {
  char* game = *reinterpret_cast<char**>(kGameGlobal);
  char* database = game == nullptr ? nullptr : Field<char*>(game, 0x140);
  if (database == nullptr) {
    return false;
  }
  const std::int32_t count = Field<std::int32_t>(database, 0x58);
  if (count <= 0) {
    return false;  // protoy.xml not read yet
  }
  if (database == database_ && count == count_) {
    return true;
  }
  database_ = database;
  count_ = count;
  ids_.clear();
  idOfName_.assign(names_.size(), -1);
  for (std::size_t i = 0; i < names_.size(); ++i) {
    const std::int32_t id = kProtoIdByName(database, names_[i].c_str());
    if (id < 0 || id >= count) {
      Log(L"%hs: %hs is not a proto in protoy.xml; ignored", feature_, names_[i].c_str());
      continue;
    }
    idOfName_[i] = id;
    ids_.push_back(id);
  }
  Log(L"%hs: %zu of %zu protos found in the data", feature_, ids_.size(), names_.size());
  return true;
}

const std::vector<std::int32_t>& ProtoIdList::Ids() {
  static const std::vector<std::int32_t> kNone;
  return Refresh() ? ids_ : kNone;
}

int ProtoIdList::IndexOf(std::int32_t proto) {
  if (proto < 0 || !Refresh()) {
    return -1;
  }
  for (std::size_t i = 0; i < idOfName_.size(); ++i) {
    if (idOfName_[i] == proto) {
      return static_cast<int>(i);
    }
  }
  return -1;
}

std::int32_t FirstCivOfPlayer(const char* player) {
  if (player == nullptr || Field<std::int32_t>(player, 0x24) <= 0) {
    return -1;
  }
  return *Field<const std::int32_t*>(player, 0x20);
}

}  // namespace uhc
```

## Models on a unit

### src/unitmodels.h

```cpp
// A model hung on a unit, as the stock aura update does (0x9D054A..0x9D059C): the rings of unitResourceAuras
// and displayedRange, and the rank marks of veterancyBonus. A model is a visual of the unit: nothing here
// reaches the simulation.
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

namespace uhc {

enum class ModelHang {
  kRetry,     // the unit has no visual yet
  kAlready,   // the unit has it
  kHung,      // on the unit now
  kNotInGame  // the engine took it and shows nothing: its file is not in the game
};

// Checks the sites of these functions, for the key's log.
bool VerifyUnitModels(const char* key);

// The ID of a model's path, as the tactics parser finds it (0x5FC91C); -1 before the table exists.
std::int32_t ModelOfPath(const wchar_t* path);

// The index that means "no such bone": the model's own file says where it goes.
std::int32_t NoBone();

ModelHang HangUnitModel(char* unit, std::int32_t model, std::int32_t bone);
bool UnitHasModel(const char* unit, std::int32_t model);
void RemoveUnitModel(char* unit, std::int32_t model);

}  // namespace uhc
```

### src/unitmodels.cpp

```cpp
#include "unitmodels.h"

#include "generated/sites.h"
#include "patch.h"

namespace {

constexpr std::uint32_t kModelTable = 0x00C66240;
constexpr std::uint32_t kBoneTable = 0x00D6F478;

using ModelIdFn = std::int32_t(__thiscall*)(void* table, const wchar_t* path, std::int32_t a, std::int32_t b, std::int32_t c);
using BoneIdFn = std::int32_t(__thiscall*)(void* table, const char* name);
using ModelByIdFn = const void*(__thiscall*)(void* table, std::int32_t model);
using HasModelFn = bool(__thiscall*)(const char* unit, std::int32_t model);
using AttachModelFn = bool(__thiscall*)(char* unit, const void* object, std::int32_t bone, std::int32_t flag);
using RemoveModelFn = void(__thiscall*)(char* unit, std::int32_t model);

const auto kModelId = reinterpret_cast<ModelIdFn>(0x0058F68F);
const auto kBoneId = reinterpret_cast<BoneIdFn>(0x0058E506);
const auto kModelById = reinterpret_cast<ModelByIdFn>(0x00405E83);
const auto kHasModel = reinterpret_cast<HasModelFn>(0x009C28DE);
const auto kAttachModel = reinterpret_cast<AttachModelFn>(0x009C04F3);
const auto kRemoveModel = reinterpret_cast<RemoveModelFn>(0x009C2885);

}  // namespace

namespace uhc {

bool VerifyUnitModels(const char* key) {
  return VerifyAll(key, {&sites::kModelById, &sites::kUnitHasModel, &sites::kUnitAttachModel, &sites::kUnitRemoveModel,
                         &sites::kModelPathLookup, &sites::kBoneNameLookup});
}

std::int32_t ModelOfPath(const wchar_t* path) {
  void* table = *reinterpret_cast<void**>(kModelTable);
  return table == nullptr ? -1 : kModelId(table, path, 1, 0, 0);
}

std::int32_t NoBone() { return kBoneId(reinterpret_cast<void*>(kBoneTable), "bonethatdoesntexist"); }

ModelHang HangUnitModel(char* unit, std::int32_t model, std::int32_t bone) {
  void* table = *reinterpret_cast<void**>(kModelTable);
  if (unit == nullptr || table == nullptr) {
    return ModelHang::kRetry;
  }
  if (kHasModel(unit, model)) {
    return ModelHang::kAlready;
  }
  const void* object = kModelById(table, model);
  if (object == nullptr || !kAttachModel(unit, object, bone, 0)) {
    return ModelHang::kRetry;
  }
  return kHasModel(unit, model) ? ModelHang::kHung : ModelHang::kNotInGame;
}

bool UnitHasModel(const char* unit, std::int32_t model) { return kHasModel(unit, model); }

void RemoveUnitModel(char* unit, std::int32_t model) { kRemoveModel(unit, model); }

}  // namespace uhc
```

## State kept for a saved game

### src/savedmarkers.h

```cpp
// State that must survive a saved game, kept on the unit itself. A marker is a stat modifier of a stat no code
// reads, with no time limit, created by 0x4DD22D. The modifier's save (slot 5Ch of vtable 0xB97C08, 0x9E3423) and
// load (0x9E355E) copy its fields as raw dwords, so four of them hold a key's words: the value [+68h] and the three
// floats of [+6Ch], which no code reads on these actions. A marker is written in place, and is reused when free,
// so a unit keeps at most as many as it ever needed at once. A transform in place (0x9C754C) ends the unit's
// modifiers, markers too: a key writes its state again at its next change.
//
// Stats: 60h and 61h transformSpan, 62h veterancyBonus, 63h and 64h damageOverTime, 65h overhealDecay, 66h
// researchGrants (two kinds, told apart by word 0).
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

#include "modifiers.h"

namespace uhc::markers {

constexpr std::uint32_t kFree = 0x3F800000;  // word 0 of a free marker: the 1.0f it is created with
constexpr int kWords = 4;

using ModifierFn = std::int32_t(__thiscall*)(char* unit, std::int32_t stat, float value, float, float, std::int32_t,
                                             std::int32_t operation, std::int32_t ms, std::int32_t, std::int32_t,
                                             std::int32_t exclusive);

inline std::uint32_t& Word(char* marker, int word) {
  constexpr std::uint32_t kOffsets[kWords] = {0x68, 0x6C, 0x70, 0x74};
  return *reinterpret_cast<std::uint32_t*>(marker + kOffsets[word]);
}

// Calls visit(marker) for each marker of the stat on the unit; visit returns false to stop.
template <typename Visit>
void ForEach(const char* unit, std::int32_t stat, Visit visit) {
  const char* list = modifiers::ModifierList(unit);
  if (list == nullptr) {
    return;
  }
  auto filter = [&](const char* action) {
    if (*reinterpret_cast<const std::int32_t*>(action + 0x64) != stat) {
      return true;
    }
    return visit(const_cast<char*>(action));
  };
  modifiers::WalkModifiers(list, filter, 0);
}

// The first marker of the stat whose word 0 is that, or nullptr.
inline char* Find(const char* unit, std::int32_t stat, std::uint32_t word0) {
  char* found = nullptr;
  ForEach(unit, stat, [&](char* marker) {
    if (Word(marker, 0) != word0) {
      return true;
    }
    found = marker;
    return false;
  });
  return found;
}

// A free marker of the stat, made if there is none; nullptr for a unit with no action manager. Its words 1 to 3
// are 0.
inline char* Take(char* unit, std::int32_t stat) {
  if (char* marker = Find(unit, stat, kFree)) {
    return marker;
  }
  const auto modifier = reinterpret_cast<ModifierFn>(0x004DD22D);
  if (modifier(unit, stat, 1.0f, -1.0f, -1.0f, 0, 0, 0, -1, -1, 0) == -1) {
    return nullptr;
  }
  return Find(unit, stat, kFree);
}

inline void Free(char* marker) {
  Word(marker, 0) = kFree;
  Word(marker, 1) = 0;
  Word(marker, 2) = 0;
  Word(marker, 3) = 0;
}

}  // namespace uhc::markers
```

## Direct3D hooks

### src/d3d9hooks.h

```cpp
// The game's Direct3D 9 entry points, owned here for every key that needs them: the IAT slot 0xB84164 of
// d3d9!Direct3DCreate9 (the exe's only d3d9 import, bound at load time, no delay load), then
// IDirect3D9::CreateDevice and IDirect3DDevice9::Reset / Present through their vtables. One owner, so two keys
// never write the same slot or vtable entry twice.
//
// rendererOption may first replace the Direct3DCreate9 the game gets (a D3D9 implementation loaded from another
// folder); the observers (perfStats, rendererOption's first-frame check) then see whichever device that creates.
// Neither changes the game's calls or their arguments.
#pragma once

#include <windows.h>

#include <d3d9.h>

namespace uhc {

using Direct3DCreate9Fn = IDirect3D9*(WINAPI*)(UINT);

// The Direct3DCreate9 the exe imports: what the IAT slot held when this was first called, checked against
// GetProcAddress of the d3d9.dll loaded with the exe. nullptr (logged with `who`) if another DLL changed the slot.
Direct3DCreate9Fn StockDirect3DCreate9(const char* who);

// The Direct3DCreate9 the game gets in the end, in place of the stock one. Once, before any AddD3D9Observer
// (rendererOption is before perfStats in kFeatures). False (logged) if the slot is not stock.
bool ReplaceDirect3DCreate9(Direct3DCreate9Fn create, const char* who);

// Each callback may be null. They run on the thread that makes the call (the game's main thread), in the order
// the observers were added.
struct D3D9Observer {
  void (*afterCreateDevice)(UINT adapter, D3DDEVTYPE type, DWORD flags, const D3DPRESENT_PARAMETERS* pp,
                            HRESULT hr, IDirect3DDevice9* device);
  void (*afterReset)(const D3DPRESENT_PARAMETERS* pp, HRESULT hr);
  void (*beforePresent)();
  void (*afterPresent)(HRESULT hr);
};

// Adds an observer. The first one points the IAT slot at the wrapper. False (logged with `who`) if the slot is
// neither stock nor what ReplaceDirect3DCreate9 wrote.
bool AddD3D9Observer(const D3D9Observer& observer, const char* who);

}  // namespace uhc
```

### src/d3d9hooks.cpp

```cpp
#include "d3d9hooks.h"

#include <cstdint>

#include "log.h"
#include "patch.h"

namespace uhc {
namespace {

// IAT slot of d3d9!Direct3DCreate9 (docs/engine-symbols.md, "IAT slots"). It holds a runtime address, so it is
// checked against GetProcAddress instead of generated/sites.h.
constexpr std::uint32_t kSlot = 0x00B84164;
constexpr int kCreateDeviceIndex = 16;  // IDirect3D9
constexpr int kResetIndex = 16;         // IDirect3DDevice9
constexpr int kPresentIndex = 17;       // IDirect3DDevice9
constexpr int kMaxObservers = 4;

using CreateDeviceFn = HRESULT(STDMETHODCALLTYPE*)(IDirect3D9*, UINT, D3DDEVTYPE, HWND, DWORD,
                                                   D3DPRESENT_PARAMETERS*, IDirect3DDevice9**);
using ResetFn = HRESULT(STDMETHODCALLTYPE*)(IDirect3DDevice9*, D3DPRESENT_PARAMETERS*);
using PresentFn = HRESULT(STDMETHODCALLTYPE*)(IDirect3DDevice9*, const RECT*, const RECT*, HWND, const RGNDATA*);

bool g_stockChecked = false;
Direct3DCreate9Fn g_stock = nullptr;
Direct3DCreate9Fn g_create = nullptr;   // what the game gets in the end: stock, or the replacement
bool g_wrapped = false;                 // the slot holds Direct3DCreate9Hook

D3D9Observer g_observers[kMaxObservers] = {};
int g_observerCount = 0;

CreateDeviceFn g_createDevice = nullptr;
ResetFn g_reset = nullptr;
PresentFn g_present = nullptr;

std::uint32_t Address(const void* p) { return static_cast<std::uint32_t>(reinterpret_cast<std::uintptr_t>(p)); }

// Points vtable entry `index` of a COM object at `hook`, keeping the original. A d3d9 implementation shares one
// vtable between all objects of a class, so a second object finds the hook already in place.
template <typename Fn>
bool HookVtable(void* object, int index, Fn hook, Fn* original) {
  void** entry = *reinterpret_cast<void***>(object) + index;
  if (*entry == reinterpret_cast<void*>(hook)) {
    return true;
  }
  *original = reinterpret_cast<Fn>(*entry);
  return WriteDword(Address(entry), Address(reinterpret_cast<void*>(hook)));
}

HRESULT STDMETHODCALLTYPE PresentHook(IDirect3DDevice9* device, const RECT* source, const RECT* dest, HWND window,
                                      const RGNDATA* dirty) {
  for (int i = 0; i < g_observerCount; ++i) {
    if (g_observers[i].beforePresent != nullptr) {
      g_observers[i].beforePresent();
    }
  }
  const HRESULT hr = g_present(device, source, dest, window, dirty);
  for (int i = 0; i < g_observerCount; ++i) {
    if (g_observers[i].afterPresent != nullptr) {
      g_observers[i].afterPresent(hr);
    }
  }
  return hr;
}

HRESULT STDMETHODCALLTYPE ResetHook(IDirect3DDevice9* device, D3DPRESENT_PARAMETERS* pp) {
  const HRESULT hr = g_reset(device, pp);
  for (int i = 0; i < g_observerCount; ++i) {
    if (g_observers[i].afterReset != nullptr) {
      g_observers[i].afterReset(pp, hr);
    }
  }
  return hr;
}

HRESULT STDMETHODCALLTYPE CreateDeviceHook(IDirect3D9* d3d, UINT adapter, D3DDEVTYPE type, HWND focus, DWORD flags,
                                           D3DPRESENT_PARAMETERS* pp, IDirect3DDevice9** device) {
  const HRESULT hr = g_createDevice(d3d, adapter, type, focus, flags, pp, device);
  IDirect3DDevice9* created = SUCCEEDED(hr) && device != nullptr ? *device : nullptr;
  if (created != nullptr && (!HookVtable(created, kPresentIndex, &PresentHook, &g_present) ||
                             !HookVtable(created, kResetIndex, &ResetHook, &g_reset))) {
    Log(L"d3d9 hooks: could not hook the device vtable; no Present or Reset observers");
  }
  for (int i = 0; i < g_observerCount; ++i) {
    if (g_observers[i].afterCreateDevice != nullptr) {
      g_observers[i].afterCreateDevice(adapter, type, flags, pp, hr, created);
    }
  }
  return hr;
}

IDirect3D9* WINAPI Direct3DCreate9Hook(UINT sdkVersion) {
  IDirect3D9* d3d = g_create(sdkVersion);
  if (d3d != nullptr && !HookVtable(d3d, kCreateDeviceIndex, &CreateDeviceHook, &g_createDevice)) {
    Log(L"d3d9 hooks: could not hook IDirect3D9::CreateDevice");
  }
  return d3d;
}

}  // namespace

Direct3DCreate9Fn StockDirect3DCreate9(const char* who) {
  if (!g_stockChecked) {
    g_stockChecked = true;
    HMODULE d3d9 = GetModuleHandleW(L"d3d9.dll");
    void* real = d3d9 != nullptr ? reinterpret_cast<void*>(GetProcAddress(d3d9, "Direct3DCreate9")) : nullptr;
    if (real != nullptr && *reinterpret_cast<void* const*>(kSlot) == real) {
      g_stock = reinterpret_cast<Direct3DCreate9Fn>(real);
      g_create = g_stock;
    }
  }
  if (g_stock == nullptr) {
    Log(L"%hs: IAT slot 0x%08X does not point at d3d9!Direct3DCreate9", who, kSlot);
  }
  return g_stock;
}

bool ReplaceDirect3DCreate9(Direct3DCreate9Fn create, const char* who) {
  if (StockDirect3DCreate9(who) == nullptr) {
    return false;
  }
  if (g_wrapped || g_create != g_stock || create == nullptr) {
    Log(L"%hs: Direct3DCreate9 is already wrapped or replaced", who);
    return false;
  }
  if (!WriteDword(kSlot, Address(reinterpret_cast<void*>(create)))) {
    return false;
  }
  g_create = create;
  return true;
}

bool AddD3D9Observer(const D3D9Observer& observer, const char* who) {
  if (StockDirect3DCreate9(who) == nullptr) {
    return false;
  }
  if (g_observerCount == kMaxObservers) {
    Log(L"%hs: too many Direct3D observers", who);
    return false;
  }
  if (!g_wrapped) {
    if (*reinterpret_cast<void* const*>(kSlot) != reinterpret_cast<void*>(g_create)) {
      Log(L"%hs: IAT slot 0x%08X was changed by another DLL", who, kSlot);
      return false;
    }
    if (!WriteDword(kSlot, Address(reinterpret_cast<void*>(&Direct3DCreate9Hook)))) {
      return false;
    }
    g_wrapped = true;
  }
  g_observers[g_observerCount++] = observer;
  return true;
}

}  // namespace uhc
```

## Research payment

### src/researchpay.h

```cpp
// The payment of a research, shared by every key that changes what a research costs (nextResearchFree,
// researchGrants): one call at each of the two payment sites (0x9E67E1 Research, 0x9D54CF CommandResearch) and
// at each of the two copies of the cost into the action for a cancel's refund (0x9E68A0, 0x9D5548).
#pragma once

namespace uhc {

// Asked before a research is paid, in the order the keys added them (their kFeatures order), until one claims
// it. `action` is the Research or CommandResearch action being set up ([action+10h] its unit; its tech is not
// written yet), `player` the paying player, `cost` the tech's cost list (a ResList). Return true to claim the
// payment with *share of the cost, 0 (free) to 1 (the full cost); the claim spends nothing yet.
using ResearchPayClaim = bool (*)(const char* action, const char* player, const char* cost, float* share);

// Called for every research that was paid and whose action is set up, claimed or not, for each key in order:
// [action+68h] is the tech ID, and the refund copy holds what was paid. `claimed` is set for the key whose claim
// it was, with its share; the others get 1. A claim whose share the player could not pay is dropped: that
// research does not start, and nothing is called.
using ResearchPayStarted = void (*)(char* action, float share, bool claimed);

// Writes the hooks the first time, after checking the stock bytes of the four sites.
bool AddResearchPayer(const char* feature, ResearchPayClaim claim, ResearchPayStarted started);

}  // namespace uhc
```

### src/researchpay.cpp

```cpp
// A research is paid when its action is set up, which is when it is queued: 0x8D946A(player, cost) at 0x9E67E1 in
// the Research action's start 0x9E667A (EBP = the action) and at 0x9D54CF in the CommandResearch action's
// 0x9D543F (ESI = the action). On success each start writes the tech ID to [action+68h], then copies the cost
// into the action for a cancel's refund: 0x480A73 at 0x9E68A0 (to +74h) and at 0x9D5548 (to +7Ch).
//
// Both payment calls go to UhcResearchPay (hooks/researchpay_hooks.asm), which asks the payers in order. With no
// claim it pays as stock. A claim with share 0 skips the payment; a share between 0 and 1 pays a scaled copy of
// the cost, built in a local list, so the tech's own cost is never written. The copy that follows gets the same
// share (0x4E2617 fills it with 0, 0x480B94 scales it), so a cancel refunds what was paid, never more.
#include "researchpay.h"

#include <cstdint>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "generated/sites.h"
#include "log.h"
#include "patch.h"

extern "C" {
void UhcResearchPayHook();
void UhcCommandResearchPayHook();
}

namespace {

constexpr std::size_t kMaxPayers = 4;
constexpr std::uint32_t kResearchCopyCall = 0x009E68A0;         // to [action+74h]
constexpr std::uint32_t kCommandResearchCopyCall = 0x009D5548;  // to [action+7Ch]
constexpr std::uint32_t kResListCount = 0x00C6DF38;             // the entries of every ResList

using PayFn = bool(__thiscall*)(void* player, const void* cost);
using CostCopyFn = void*(__thiscall*)(void* cost, const void* from);
using CostFillFn = void(__thiscall*)(void* cost, float value);
using CostScaleFn = void(__thiscall*)(void* cost, float factor);
const auto kPay = reinterpret_cast<PayFn>(0x008D946A);
const auto kCostCopy = reinterpret_cast<CostCopyFn>(0x00480A73);
const auto kCostFill = reinterpret_cast<CostFillFn>(0x004E2617);
const auto kCostScale = reinterpret_cast<CostScaleFn>(0x00480B94);

struct Payer {
  uhc::ResearchPayClaim claim;
  uhc::ResearchPayStarted started;
};

Payer g_payers[kMaxPayers] = {};
std::size_t g_payerCount = 0;
bool g_installed = false;

// The payment just claimed and paid, for the copy that follows it.
const Payer* g_pending = nullptr;
const char* g_pendingAction = nullptr;
float g_pendingShare = 1.0f;

// A ResList laid out as the game's: +0 the encoded entries, +4 the encoded total.
struct ResList {
  std::uint32_t* entries;
  std::uint32_t total;
};

bool PayShare(void* player, const void* cost, float share) {
  if (share <= 0.0f) {
    return true;
  }
  const auto* list = static_cast<const ResList*>(cost);
  const std::int32_t count = *reinterpret_cast<const std::int32_t*>(kResListCount);
  if (share >= 1.0f || list == nullptr || list->entries == nullptr || count <= 0) {
    return kPay(player, cost);
  }
  std::vector<std::uint32_t> entries(list->entries, list->entries + count);
  ResList scaled{entries.data(), list->total};
  kCostScale(&scaled, share);
  return kPay(player, &scaled);
}

void* CopyCost(void* cost, const void* from, std::uint32_t offset) {
  void* result = kCostCopy(cost, from);
  char* action = static_cast<char*>(cost) - offset;
  const Payer* payer = action == g_pendingAction ? g_pending : nullptr;
  const float share = payer != nullptr ? g_pendingShare : 1.0f;
  g_pending = nullptr;
  g_pendingAction = nullptr;
  if (share <= 0.0f) {
    kCostFill(cost, 0.0f);
  } else if (share < 1.0f) {
    kCostScale(cost, share);
  }
  for (std::size_t i = 0; i < g_payerCount; ++i) {
    const bool claimed = payer == &g_payers[i];
    g_payers[i].started(action, claimed ? share : 1.0f, claimed);
  }
  return result;
}

void* __fastcall CopyResearchCost(void* cost, void*, const void* from) { return CopyCost(cost, from, 0x74); }
void* __fastcall CopyCommandResearchCost(void* cost, void*, const void* from) { return CopyCost(cost, from, 0x7C); }

}  // namespace

// From the two hooks, instead of the payment. The action is the one being set up.
extern "C" bool __stdcall UhcResearchPay(const char* action, void* player, const void* cost) {
  g_pending = nullptr;
  for (std::size_t i = 0; i < g_payerCount; ++i) {
    float share = 1.0f;
    if (!g_payers[i].claim(action, static_cast<const char*>(player), static_cast<const char*>(cost), &share)) {
      continue;
    }
    share = share < 0.0f ? 0.0f : (share > 1.0f ? 1.0f : share);
    if (!PayShare(player, cost, share)) {
      return false;  // the research does not start; nothing was spent
    }
    g_pending = &g_payers[i];
    g_pendingAction = action;
    g_pendingShare = share;
    return true;
  }
  return kPay(player, cost);
}

namespace uhc {

bool AddResearchPayer(const char* feature, ResearchPayClaim claim, ResearchPayStarted started) {
  if (g_payerCount == kMaxPayers) {
    Log(L"research payment: more than %u keys", static_cast<unsigned>(kMaxPayers));
    return false;
  }
  if (!g_installed) {
    if (!VerifyAll(feature, {&sites::kResearchPayCall, &sites::kCommandResearchPayCall,
                             &sites::kResearchCostCopyCall, &sites::kCommandResearchCostCopyCall,
                             &sites::kResListCopy, &sites::kResListScale}) ||
        !WriteCall(sites::kResearchPayCall.va, reinterpret_cast<const void*>(&UhcResearchPayHook)) ||
        !WriteCall(sites::kCommandResearchPayCall.va, reinterpret_cast<const void*>(&UhcCommandResearchPayHook)) ||
        !WriteCall(kResearchCopyCall, reinterpret_cast<const void*>(&CopyResearchCost)) ||
        !WriteCall(kCommandResearchCopyCall, reinterpret_cast<const void*>(&CopyCommandResearchCost))) {
      return false;
    }
    g_installed = true;
  }
  g_payers[g_payerCount++] = {claim, started};
  return true;
}

}  // namespace uhc
```

### src/hooks/researchpay_hooks.asm

```asm
; The research payment (src/researchpay.cpp), shared by nextResearchFree and researchGrants. Both replace a call
; of the payment 0x8D946A, whose argument, the cost, is already on the stack, and return to the stock code as
; that call did: al, ret 4.
.686
.model flat, c
option casemap :none

include common.inc

UhcResearchPay PROTO STDCALL action:DWORD, player:DWORD, cost:DWORD

.code

; The call at 0x9E67E1 in the Research action's start. ECX = the player, EBP = the action.
UhcResearchPayHook PROC
    TRACE_ONCE "research payment (0x9E67E1)"
    mov eax, dword ptr [esp+4]
    invoke UhcResearchPay, ebp, ecx, eax
    ret 4
UhcResearchPayHook ENDP

; The call at 0x9D54CF in the CommandResearch action's start. ECX = the player, ESI = the action.
UhcCommandResearchPayHook PROC
    TRACE_ONCE "command research payment (0x9D54CF)"
    mov eax, dword ptr [esp+4]
    invoke UhcResearchPay, esi, ecx, eax
    ret 4
UhcCommandResearchPayHook ENDP

END
```
